   540	    fun handleSystemBack(): Boolean {
   541	        if (activeMockFlow == null || mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress <= 0f) return false
   542	        closeMockSignInScreen()
   543	        return true
   544	    }
   545	
   546	    fun restoreIntroSnapshot(snapshot: CinerificIntroSnapshot) {
   547	        if (appliedIntroSnapshot == snapshot) return
   548	
   549	        val restoredFlow = enumValueOrNull<MockAccountFlow>(snapshot.activeFlowName)
   550	        val restoredRecoveryTarget = enumValueOrNull<MockForgotRecoveryTarget>(
   551	            snapshot.forgotRecoveryTargetName
   552	        ) ?: MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password
   553	        val restoredSubmissionState = enumValueOrNull<MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState>(
   554	            snapshot.forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName
   555	        ) ?: MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Idle
   556	
   557	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   558	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   559	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   560	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   561	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   562	        pressedMockField = null
   563	        pressedRememberMe = false
   564	        pressedBackChevron = false
   565	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   566	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   567	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   568	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   569	        lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   570	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   571	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   572	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   573	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   574	        pressedCreateAccountContinue = false
   575	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   576	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   577	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   578	        activeComposingText = ""
   579	        expandedMockDropdown = null
   580	        focusedMockField = null
   581	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
   582	        mockInputDimActiveField = null
   583	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
   584	
   585	        activeMockFlow = restoredFlow
   586	        if (restoredFlow == null) {
   587	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
   588	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
   589	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
   590	        } else {
   591	            mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   592	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
   593	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
   594	        }
   595	
   596	        rememberMeChecked = snapshot.rememberMeChecked
   597	        usernameText = snapshot.usernameText
   598	        mockCreateUsernameRevealProgress = if (usernameText.isEmpty()) 0f else 1f
   599	        passwordText = snapshot.passwordText
   600	        confirmPasswordText = snapshot.confirmPasswordText
   601	        emailText = snapshot.emailText
   602	        confirmEmailText = snapshot.confirmEmailText
   603	        monthText = snapshot.monthText
   604	        dayText = snapshot.dayText
   605	        yearText = snapshot.yearText
   606	        subscriptionTierText = normalizedMembershipTierSelection(snapshot.subscriptionTierText)
   607	        forgotRecoveryTarget = restoredRecoveryTarget
   608	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = restoredSubmissionState
   609	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = if (
   610	            restoredSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading
   611	        ) {
   612	            SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   613	        } else {
   614	            null
   615	        }
   616	
   617	        mockCreateAvatarIndex = snapshot.createAvatarIndex.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)
   618	        portraitProfileIndex = snapshot.portraitProfileIndex.coerceIn(0, FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.lastIndex)
   619	        resetPortraitProfileCarousel()
   620	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartMillis = null
   621	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
   622	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex
   623	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection = MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next
   624	        mockCreateAvatarCarouselStartProgress = 0f
   625	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets.keys.forEach { dropdown ->
   626	            mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = 0
   627	        }
   628	
   629	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
   630	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
   631	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   632	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
   633	        appliedIntroSnapshot = currentIntroSnapshot()
   634	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   635	    }
   636	
   637	    override fun onSizeChanged(width: Int, height: Int, oldWidth: Int, oldHeight: Int) {
   638	        super.onSizeChanged(width, height, oldWidth, oldHeight)
   639	        pressedAvatarProfile = null
   640	        clickAvatarProfile = null
   641	        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
   642	        pressedSignInPrompt = false
   643	        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
   644	        pressedMockField = null
   645	        pressedRememberMe = false
   646	        pressedBackChevron = false
   647	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   648	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   649	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   650	        pressedMockDropdown = null
   651	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   652	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
   653	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
   654	        pressedCreateAccountContinue = false
   655	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   656	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   657	        mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   658	        resetPortraitProfileCarousel()
   659	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   660	    }
   661	
   662	    override fun onApplyWindowInsets(insets: WindowInsets): WindowInsets {
   663	        val systemBars = WindowInsetsCompat.toWindowInsetsCompat(insets, this)
   664	            .getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.systemBars())
   665	        systemBarInsetTopPx = systemBars.top
   666	        systemBarInsetBottomPx = systemBars.bottom
   667	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   668	        return super.onApplyWindowInsets(insets)
   669	    }
   670	
   671	    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
   672	        super.onDraw(canvas)
   673	
   674	        if (IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis == 0L) {
   675	            IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
   676	        }
   677	        val progress = bootProgressAt(IntroAnimationClock.processStartMillis)
   678	        val blackToPurple = linearSegmentMs(
   679	            progress,
   680	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS,
   681	            SCREEN_BLACK_HOLD_MS + SCREEN_PINK_MS
   682	        )
   683	        val purpleToSettle = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2500, 3900)
   684	        drawIntroBackground(canvas, blackToPurple, purpleToSettle)
   685	
   686	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return
   687	        finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded()
   688	        finishMockFlowSwitchIfNeeded()
   689	        finishPortraitProfileCarouselIfNeeded()
   690	        finishMockCreateAvatarCarouselIfNeeded()
   691	        finishMockForgotPasswordSubmissionIfNeeded()
   692	        val mockProgress = mockSignInProgress()
   693	        updateMockFormFocusAnimation()
   694	        updateMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
   695	        updateMockInputDimAnimation()
   696	        updateMockCreateUsernameRevealAnimation()
   697	        val formFocusMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress)
   698	
   699	        val logoAlpha = linearSegmentMs(progress, 90, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS)
   700	        if (logoAlpha > 0.01f) {
   701	            drawSettlingLogo(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, progress, logoAlpha)
   702	        }
   703	
   704	        val avatarAlpha = easedSegmentMs(progress, 2300, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
   705	        val mockMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockProgress)
   706	        val createWelcomeTransition = isMockCreateWelcomeTransition()
   707	        if (avatarAlpha > 0.01f) {
   708	            val stackAlpha = avatarAlpha * (1f - mockMotion)
   709	            val y = lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha) + MOCK_SIGN_IN_STACK_EXIT_Y * mockMotion
   710	            if (!createWelcomeTransition && stackAlpha > 0.01f) {
   711	                drawWelcomeAvatarStack(canvas, stage, y, stackAlpha)
   712	            }
   713	        }
   714	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   715	            val outgoingFlow = outgoingMockFlow
   716	            if (outgoingFlow != null) {
   717	                val switchMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFlowSwitchProgress())
   718	                drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
   719	                    canvas,
   720	                    outgoingFlow,
   721	                    stage.left,
   722	                    stage.top,
   723	                    stage.scale,
   724	                    1f - switchMotion,
   725	                    formFocusMotion,
   726	                    updateLabelAnimation = false
   727	                )
   728	                activeMockFlow?.let { incomingFlow ->
   729	                    drawMockSignInFormForFlow(
   730	                        canvas,
   731	                        incomingFlow,
   732	                        stage.left,
   733	                        stage.top,
   734	                        stage.scale,
   735	                        switchMotion,
   736	                        formFocusMotion,
   737	                        updateLabelAnimation = true
   738	                    )
   739	                }
   740	            } else {
   741	                drawMockSignInForm(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, mockMotion, formFocusMotion)
   742	            }
   743	        }
   744	        if (
   745	            logoAlpha > 0.01f &&
   746	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.SignIn &&
   747	            usesStickyAuthChrome()
   748	        ) {
   749	            drawSettlingLogo(canvas, stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, progress, logoAlpha)
   750	        }
   751	        drawAccountPrompt(
   752	            canvas = canvas,
   753	            stageLeft = stage.left,
   754	            stageTop = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) stage.top else accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale),
   755	            stageScale = stage.scale,
   756	            yOffset = when {
   757	                mockSignInStartMillis == null -> lerpFloat(56f, 0f, avatarAlpha)
   758	                isPhoneLandscapeWelcomeLayout() -> MOCK_SIGN_IN_STACK_EXIT_Y * mockMotion
   759	                else -> 0f
   760	            },
   761	            alpha = if (isPhoneLandscapeWelcomeLayout()) {
   762	                avatarAlpha * (1f - mockMotion)
   763	            } else {
   764	                avatarAlpha
   765	            }
   766	        )
   767	        drawMockCreateAvatarTransitionOverlay(
   768	            canvas,
   769	            stage,
   770	            mockMotion,
   771	            formFocusMotion
   772	        )
   773	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   774	            drawPortraitStickyBottomBannerForeground(
   775	                canvas,
   776	                stage,
   777	                blackToPurple,
   778	                purpleToSettle
   779	            )
   780	            drawBackChevron(
   781	                canvas,
   782	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X,
   783	                mockBackChevronRowTopY(),
   784	                stage.left,
   785	                if (usesStickyAuthChrome()) {
   786	                    stage.top
   787	                } else {
   788	                    accountPromptStageTop(stage.scale)
   789	                },
   790	                stage.scale,
   791	                mockMotion
   792	            )
   793	        }
   794	
   795	        if (
   796	            progress < 1f ||
   797	            isMockSignInAnimating() ||
   798	            isMockFlowSwitchAnimating() ||
   799	            isMockFormLabelAnimating() ||
   800	            isMockFormFocusAnimating() ||
   801	            isMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimating() ||
   802	            isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating() ||
   803	            isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating() ||
   804	            isMockForgotPasswordSubmissionAnimating() ||
   805	            isMockInputDimAnimating() ||
   806	            isMockCreateUsernameRevealAnimating()
   807	        ) {
   808	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   809	        }
   810	    }
   811	
   812	    override fun onTouchEvent(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
   813	        return when (event.actionMasked) {
   814	            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
   815	                mockTouchDownX = event.x
   816	                mockTouchDownY = event.y
   817	                mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = false
   818	                portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
   819	                portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
   820	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   821	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
   822	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   823	                    portraitAuthScrollStartY = event.y
   824	                    portraitAuthScrollStartOffset = portraitAuthScrollY
   825	                    portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
   826	                    if (isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) {
   827	                        return true
   828	                    }
   829	                    mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = isMockAuthScreenAtBottom()
   830	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   831	                    pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   832	                        settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   833	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   834	                        !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   835	                        settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   836	                    if (
   837	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   838	                        pressedSignInPrompt ||
   839	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt
   840	                    ) {
   841	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   842	                        return true
   843	                    }
   844	                    val touchedDropdownOptions = mockDropdownOptionsHit(event.x, event.y)
   845	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = touchedDropdownOptions?.takeIf { mockDropdownCanScroll(it) }
   846	                    mockDropdownScrollStartY = event.y
   847	                    mockDropdownScrollStartOffset = activeMockDropdownScroll?.let { mockDropdownScrollOffset(it) } ?: 0
   848	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = if (touchedDropdownOptions != null) {
   849	                        mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
   850	                    } else {
   851	                        null
   852	                    }
   853	                    pressedMockDropdown = if (pressedMockDropdownOption == null && touchedDropdownOptions == null) {
   854	                        mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
   855	                    } else {
   856	                        null
   857	                    }
   858	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = if (
   859	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   860	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   861	                        pressedMockDropdown == null
   862	                    ) {
   863	                        mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
   864	                    } else {
   865	                        null
   866	                    }
   867	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   868	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   869	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   870	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   871	                        mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
   872	                    pressedCreateAccountContinue = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   873	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   874	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   875	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   876	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   877	                        mockCreateAccountContinueHit(event.x, event.y)
   878	                    val touchedCreateAvatarCarousel = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   879	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   880	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   881	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   882	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   883	                        !pressedCreateAccountContinue &&
   884	                        mockCreateAvatarCarouselHit(event.x, event.y) &&
   885	                        !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
   886	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   887	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   888	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
   889	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
   890	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = if (
   891	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   892	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   893	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   894	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   895	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   896	                        touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   897	                    ) {
   898	                        mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
   899	                    } else {
   900	                        null
   901	                    }
   902	                    pressedMockField = if (
   903	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   904	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   905	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   906	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   907	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   908	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   909	                        !pressedCreateAccountContinue &&
   910	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel
   911	                    ) {
   912	                        mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
   913	                    } else {
   914	                        null
   915	                    }
   916	                    pressedRememberMe = touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   917	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   918	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   919	                        pressedMockField == null &&
   920	                        mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
   921	                    pressedBackChevron = pressedMockField == null &&
   922	                        touchedDropdownOptions == null &&
   923	                        !touchedCreateAvatarCarousel &&
   924	                        !pressedRememberMe &&
   925	                        pressedMockDropdown == null &&
   926	                        pressedMockDropdownOption == null &&
   927	                        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == null &&
   928	                        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == null &&
   929	                        !pressedForgotPasswordSubmit &&
   930	                        !pressedCreateAccountContinue &&
   931	                        mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
   932	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   933	                    return true
   934	                }
   935	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   936	                pressedSignInPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt && settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   937	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = !pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
   938	                    !pressedSignInPrompt &&
   939	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
   940	                pressedAvatarProfile = if (
   941	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
   942	                    pressedSignInPrompt ||
   943	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
   944	                    isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()
   945	                ) {
   946	                    null
   947	                } else {
   948	                    settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
   949	                }
   950	                // This custom view owns the entire welcome surface. Returning false for a
   951	                // slightly imprecise DOWN lets AndroidView's parent cancel the rest of the
   952	                // gesture, so a valid UP never reaches the link or avatar hit test.
   953	                true
   954	            }
   955	            MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
   956	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
   957	                    if (updatePortraitAuthScroll(event)) {
   958	                        return true
   959	                    }
   960	                    if (updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event)) {
   961	                        return true
   962	                    }
   963	                    if (updateMockDropdownScroll(event)) {
   964	                        return true
   965	                    }
   966	                    if (mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) == null && shouldStartMockDragReturn(event)) {
   967	                        mockDragReturnInProgress = true
   968	                        portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
   969	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
   970	                    }
   971	                    return true
   972	                }
   973	                if (isPortraitIntroLayout() && pressedAvatarProfile != null) {
   974	                    val horizontalDelta = event.x - mockTouchDownX
   975	                    val verticalDelta = event.y - mockTouchDownY
   976	                    if (abs(horizontalDelta) > touchSlop && abs(horizontalDelta) > abs(verticalDelta)) {
   977	                        portraitProfileSwipeMoved = true
   978	                    }
   979	                    if (portraitProfileSwipeMoved) {
   980	                        portraitProfileDragDeltaX = horizontalDelta
   981	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
   982	                    }
   983	                    return true
   984	                }
   985	                true
   986	            }
   987	            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP -> {
   988	                mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = false
   989	                if (portraitAuthScrollMoved) {
   990	                    portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
   991	                    pressedMockField = null
   992	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
   993	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
   994	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
   995	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
   996	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
   997	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
   998	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
   999	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1000	                    return true
  1001	                }
  1002	                if (mockDragReturnInProgress) {
  1003	                    mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  1004	                    pressedMockField = null
  1005	                    pressedRememberMe = false
  1006	                    pressedBackChevron = false
  1007	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  1008	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  1009	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  1010	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  1011	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
  1012	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
  1013	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  1014	                    pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  1015	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  1016	                    pressedCreateAccountContinue = false
  1017	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  1018	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  1019	                    return true
  1020	                }
  1021	                if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) {
  1022	                    if (isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()) {
  1023	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
  1024	                        pressedSignInPrompt = false
  1025	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
  1026	                        return true
  1027	                    }
  1028	                    val requestedFlow = when {
  1029	                        pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
  1030	                            settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
  1031	                            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
  1032	                        }
  1033	                        pressedSignInPrompt &&
  1034	                            settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
  1035	                            MockAccountFlow.SignIn
  1036	                        }
  1037	                        pressedForgotPasswordPrompt &&
  1038	                            settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y) -> {
  1039	                            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  1040	                        }
  1041	                        else -> null
  1042	                    }
  1043	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
  1044	                    pressedSignInPrompt = false
  1045	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
  1046	                    if (requestedFlow != null) {
  1047	                        super.performClick()
  1048	                        switchMockAccountFlow(requestedFlow)
  1049	                        return true
  1050	                    }
  1051	                    val releasedDropdownOption = mockDropdownOptionHit(event.x, event.y)
  1052	                    val releasedDropdown = mockDropdownHit(event.x, event.y)
  1053	                    val releasedField = mockSignInFieldHit(event.x, event.y)
  1054	                    val releasedRememberMe = mockRememberMeHit(event.x, event.y)
  1055	                    val releasedBackChevron = mockBackChevronHit(event.x, event.y)
  1056	                    val releasedCreateAvatarNav = mockCreateAvatarNavHit(event.x, event.y)
  1057	                    val releasedForgotRecoveryTarget = mockForgotRecoveryTargetHit(event.x, event.y)
  1058	                    val releasedForgotPasswordSubmit = mockForgotPasswordSubmitHit(event.x, event.y)
  1059	                    val releasedCreateAccountContinue = mockCreateAccountContinueHit(event.x, event.y)
  1060	                    val wasTouchingDropdownOptions = activeMockDropdownScroll != null ||
  1061	                        mockDropdownOptionsHit(mockTouchDownX, mockTouchDownY) != null
  1062	                    val wasScrollingDropdown = mockDropdownScrollMoved
  1063	                    val wasPressingRememberMe = pressedRememberMe
  1064	                    val wasPressingBackChevron = pressedBackChevron
  1065	                    val wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget != null
  1066	                    val wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit
  1067	                    val wasPressingCreateAccountContinue = pressedCreateAccountContinue
  1068	                    val wasDraggingCreateAvatar = activeMockCreateAvatarDrag
  1069	                    val createAvatarDragMoved = mockCreateAvatarDragMoved
  1070	                    val createAvatarDragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
  1071	                    val createAvatarDragProgress = mockCreateAvatarDragProgress()
  1072	                    val pressedDropdown = pressedMockDropdown
  1073	                    val pressedOption = pressedMockDropdownOption
  1074	                    val pressedCreateAvatarNav = pressedMockCreateAvatarNav
  1075	                    val pressedForgotRecoverySelection = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget
  1076	                    val shouldSelectDropdownOption = !wasScrollingDropdown &&
  1077	                        pressedOption != null &&
  1078	                        pressedOption == releasedDropdownOption
  1079	                    val shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget = pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null &&
  1080	                        pressedForgotRecoverySelection == releasedForgotRecoveryTarget
  1081	                    val shouldToggleDropdown = pressedDropdown != null && pressedDropdown == releasedDropdown
  1082	                    val shouldToggleRememberMe = wasPressingRememberMe && releasedRememberMe
  1083	                    val shouldCloseMockSignIn = wasPressingBackChevron && releasedBackChevron
  1084	                    val shouldSubmitForgotPassword = wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit && releasedForgotPasswordSubmit
  1085	                    val shouldContinueCreateAccount = wasPressingCreateAccountContinue &&
  1086	                        releasedCreateAccountContinue
  1087	                    val shouldNavigateCreateAvatar = pressedCreateAvatarNav != null &&
  1088	                        !createAvatarDragMoved &&
  1089	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == releasedCreateAvatarNav
  1090	                    val shouldSwipeCreateAvatar = wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
  1091	                        createAvatarDragMoved &&
  1092	                        createAvatarDragDirection != null &&
  1093	                        createAvatarDragProgress >= MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_DRAG_COMMIT_PROGRESS
  1094	                    val shouldFocusField = releasedField != null &&
  1095	                        !wasTouchingDropdownOptions &&
  1096	                        !wasPressingRememberMe &&
  1097	                        !wasPressingBackChevron &&
  1098	                        !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget &&
  1099	                        !wasPressingForgotPasswordSubmit &&
  1100	                        !wasPressingCreateAccountContinue &&
  1101	                        !wasDraggingCreateAvatar &&
  1102	                        pressedDropdown == null &&
  1103	                        pressedOption == null &&
  1104	                        pressedCreateAvatarNav == null &&
  1105	                        (pressedMockField == null || pressedMockField == releasedField)
  1106	                    pressedMockField = null
  1107	                    pressedRememberMe = false
  1108	                    pressedBackChevron = false
  1109	                    pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  1110	                    activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  1111	                    mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  1112	                    mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  1113	                    lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
  1114	                    pressedMockDropdown = null
  1115	                    pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  1116	                    this.pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  1117	                    pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  1118	                    pressedCreateAccountContinue = false
  1119	                    activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  1120	                    mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  1121	                    super.performClick()
  1122	                    if (shouldCloseMockSignIn) {
  1123	                        closeMockSignInScreen()
  1124	                    } else if (shouldSwipeCreateAvatar && createAvatarDragDirection != null) {
  1125	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1126	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1127	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(createAvatarDragDirection, createAvatarDragProgress)
  1128	                    } else if (shouldNavigateCreateAvatar && pressedCreateAvatarNav != null) {
  1129	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1130	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1131	                        startMockCreateAvatarCarousel(pressedCreateAvatarNav)
  1132	                    } else if (wasDraggingCreateAvatar) {
  1133	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1134	                    } else if (shouldSelectDropdownOption && pressedOption != null) {
  1135	                        setMockDropdownValue(pressedOption)
  1136	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1137	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1138	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1139	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1140	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1141	                    } else if (shouldToggleDropdown && pressedDropdown != null) {
  1142	                        expandedMockDropdown = if (expandedMockDropdown == pressedDropdown) null else pressedDropdown
  1143	                        expandedMockDropdown?.let { prepareMockDropdownScroll(it) }
  1144	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1145	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1146	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1147	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1148	                    } else if (shouldToggleRememberMe) {
  1149	                        rememberMeChecked = !rememberMeChecked
  1150	                        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  1151	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1152	                    } else if (shouldSelectForgotRecoveryTarget && pressedForgotRecoverySelection != null) {
  1153	                        setMockForgotRecoveryTarget(pressedForgotRecoverySelection)
  1154	                    } else if (shouldSubmitForgotPassword) {
  1155	                        startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  1156	                    } else if (shouldContinueCreateAccount) {
  1157	                        if (mockCreateAccountCanContinue()) {
  1158	                            showCreateAccountValidationWarning = false
  1159	                            expandedMockDropdown = null
  1160	                            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1161	                            onCreateAccountContinue?.invoke()
  1162	                        } else {
  1163	                            showCreateAccountValidationWarning = true
  1164	                            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1165	                        }
  1166	                    } else if (shouldFocusField && releasedField != null) {
  1167	                        expandedMockDropdown = null
  1168	                        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1169	                        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1170	                        focusMockSignInField(releasedField)
  1171	                    } else if (wasTouchingDropdownOptions || wasScrollingDropdown) {
  1172	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1173	                    } else if (!wasPressingRememberMe && !wasPressingBackChevron && !wasPressingForgotRecoveryTarget) {
  1174	                        if (expandedMockDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1175	                            expandedMockDropdown = null
  1176	                            restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  1177	                            restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  1178	                        }
  1179	                        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  1180	                    } else {
  1181	                        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1182	                    }
  1183	                    return true
  1184	                }
  1185	                val tapStayedWithinSlop =
  1186	                    abs(event.x - mockTouchDownX) <= touchSlop &&
  1187	                        abs(event.y - mockTouchDownY) <= touchSlop
  1188	                val shouldOpenMockCreateAccount = tapStayedWithinSlop &&
  1189	                    pressedCreateAccountPrompt &&
  1190	                    settledCreateAccountPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1191	                val shouldOpenMockSignIn = tapStayedWithinSlop &&
  1192	                    pressedSignInPrompt &&
  1193	                    settledSignInPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1194	                val shouldOpenMockForgotPassword = tapStayedWithinSlop &&
  1195	                    pressedForgotPasswordPrompt &&
  1196	                    settledForgotPasswordPromptHit(event.x, event.y)
  1197	                val releasedAvatarProfile = settledAvatarHitProfile(event.x, event.y)
  1198	                val swipeDirection = portraitProfileSwipeDirection()
  1199	                val swipeProgress = portraitProfileDragProgress()
  1200	                val shouldSwipeProfile = isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  1201	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null &&
  1202	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved &&
  1203	                    swipeProgress >= PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_COMMIT_PROGRESS &&
  1204	                    swipeDirection != null
  1205	                val shouldNavigate = tapStayedWithinSlop &&
  1206	                    !shouldSwipeProfile &&
  1207	                    !portraitProfileSwipeMoved &&
  1208	                    pressedAvatarProfile != null &&
  1209	                    pressedAvatarProfile == releasedAvatarProfile
  1210	                clickAvatarProfile = releasedAvatarProfile
  1211	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
  1212	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
  1213	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
  1214	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
  1215	                if (shouldSwipeProfile && swipeDirection != null) {
  1216	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
  1217	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1218	                    portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1219	                    startPortraitProfileCarousel(swipeDirection, swipeProgress)
  1220	                    true
  1221	                } else if (shouldOpenMockCreateAccount) {
  1222	                    super.performClick()
  1223	                    openMockCreateAccountScreen()
  1224	                    true
  1225	                } else if (shouldOpenMockSignIn) {
  1226	                    super.performClick()
  1227	                    openMockSignInScreen()
  1228	                    true
  1229	                } else if (shouldOpenMockForgotPassword) {
  1230	                    super.performClick()
  1231	                    openMockForgotPasswordScreen()
  1232	                    true
  1233	                } else if (shouldNavigate) {
  1234	                    performClick()
  1235	                    true
  1236	                } else {
  1237	                    clickAvatarProfile = null
  1238	                    portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1239	                    portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1240	                    postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  1241	                    true
  1242	                }
  1243	            }
  1244	            MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
  1245	                pressedAvatarProfile = null
  1246	                clickAvatarProfile = null
  1247	                pressedCreateAccountPrompt = false
  1248	                pressedSignInPrompt = false
  1249	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt = false
  1250	                pressedMockField = null
  1251	                pressedRememberMe = false
  1252	                pressedBackChevron = false
  1253	                pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  1254	                activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  1255	                mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  1256	                mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  1257	                lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
  1258	                pressedMockDropdown = null
  1259	                pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  1260	                pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  1261	                pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  1262	                pressedCreateAccountContinue = false
  1263	                activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  1264	                mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  1265	                mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  1266	                mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = false
  1267	                portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
  1268	                portraitProfileSwipeMoved = false
  1269	                portraitProfileDragDeltaX = 0f
  1270	                true
  1271	            }
  1272	            else -> pressedCreateAccountPrompt ||
  1273	                pressedSignInPrompt ||
  1274	                pressedForgotPasswordPrompt ||
  1275	                pressedAvatarProfile != null ||
  1276	                mockSignInStartMillis != null
  1277	        }
  1278	    }
  1279	
  1280	    override fun onCheckIsTextEditor(): Boolean {
  1281	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && !isMockSignInClosing()
  1282	    }
  1283	
  1284	    override fun onCreateInputConnection(outAttrs: EditorInfo): InputConnection? {
  1285	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return null
  1286	        outAttrs.inputType = when (field) {
  1287	            MockSignInField.Password,
  1288	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_PASSWORD
  1289	            MockSignInField.Email,
  1290	            MockSignInField.ConfirmEmail -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_EMAIL_ADDRESS
  1291	            MockSignInField.Month,
  1292	            MockSignInField.Day,
  1293	            MockSignInField.Year -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_NUMBER
  1294	            else -> InputType.TYPE_CLASS_TEXT or InputType.TYPE_TEXT_VARIATION_NORMAL
  1295	        }
  1296	        val action = if (field == activeMockFields().lastOrNull()) {
  1297	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE
  1298	        } else {
  1299	            EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_NEXT
  1300	        }
  1301	        outAttrs.imeOptions = action or
  1302	            EditorInfo.IME_FLAG_NO_EXTRACT_UI or
  1303	            EditorInfo.IME_FLAG_NO_FULLSCREEN
  1304	        val textLength = mockSignInFieldText(field).length
  1305	        outAttrs.initialSelStart = textLength
  1306	        outAttrs.initialSelEnd = textLength
  1307	        return MockSignInInputConnection()
  1308	    }
  1309	
  1310	    override fun onKeyDown(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1311	        if (focusedMockField != null) {
  1312	            return handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode, event) || super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  1313	        }
  1314	        return super.onKeyDown(keyCode, event)
  1315	    }
  1316	
  1317	    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
  1318	    override fun onKeyMultiple(keyCode: Int, repeatCount: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  1319	        val characters = event.getCharacters()
  1320	        if (focusedMockField != null && !characters.isNullOrEmpty()) {
  1321	            return appendMockSignInInput(characters)
  1322	        }
  1323	        return super.onKeyMultiple(keyCode, repeatCount, event)
  1324	    }
  1325	
  1326	    override fun performClick(): Boolean {
  1327	        super.performClick()
  1328	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return false
  1329	        val profile = clickAvatarProfile ?: return false
  1330	        onAvatarSelected?.invoke(profile)
  1331	        clickAvatarProfile = null
  1332	        return true
  1333	    }

  1335	    private fun drawSettlingLogo(
  1336	        canvas: Canvas,
  1337	        stageLeft: Float,
  1338	        stageTop: Float,
  1339	        stageScale: Float,
  1340	        progress: Float,
  1341	        alpha: Float
  1342	    ) {
  1343	        val logoEntry = bouncySegmentMs(progress, LOGO_ENTRY_START_MS, LOGO_ENTRY_END_MS)
  1344	        val finalProgress = easedSegmentMs(progress, FINAL_SETTLE_START_MS, FINAL_SETTLE_END_MS)
  1345	        val focusShiftY = mockLogoFocusShiftY()
  1346	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  1347	        val portraitLogo = Bounds(
  1348	            (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - 600f * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE) / 2f +
  1349	                PORTRAIT_LOGO_VISIBLE_CENTER_OFFSET_X,
  1350	            portraitLogoTop(),
  1351	            600f * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE,
  1352	            LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE
  1353	        )
  1354	        val portraitCenteredLogo = Bounds(
  1355	            x = portraitLogo.x + portraitLogo.w / 2f -
  1356	                portraitLogo.w * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE / 2f,
  1357	            y = portraitStackCenter(0.5f) -
  1358	                portraitLogo.h * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE / 2f,
  1359	            w = portraitLogo.w * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE,
  1360	            h = portraitLogo.h * LOGO_PRE_SETTLE_SCALE
  1361	        )
  1362	        val authLogoScale = landscapeAuthLogoScale()
  1363	        val base = scaleBoundsFromTopCenter(
  1364	            offsetBounds(
  1365	                lerpBounds(Bounds(222f, 150f, 750f, 535f), portraitCenteredLogo, portraitAmount),
  1366	                focusShiftY
  1367	            ),
  1368	            authLogoScale
  1369	        )
  1370	        val final = scaleBoundsFromTopCenter(
  1371	            offsetBounds(
  1372	                lerpBounds(
  1373	                    Bounds(297f, LOGO_FINAL_TOP, 600f, LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT),
  1374	                    portraitLogo,
  1375	                    portraitAmount
  1376	                ),
  1377	                focusShiftY
  1378	            ),
  1379	            authLogoScale
  1380	        )
  1381	
  1382	        if (logoEntry >= 0.999f) {
  1383	            drawFigmaBitmap(
  1384	                canvas,
  1385	                logoCombined,
  1386	                phoneLandscapeWelcomeBounds(lerpBounds(base, final, finalProgress)),
  1387	                stageLeft,
  1388	                stageTop,
  1389	                stageScale,
  1390	                alpha
  1391	            )
  1392	            return
  1393	        }
  1394	
  1395	        val simpleEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(447f, 1037f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
  1396	        val eyesEntry = lerpBounds(Bounds(453f, -417f, 300f, 214f), base, logoEntry)
  1397	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoSimple, phoneLandscapeWelcomeBounds(lerpBounds(simpleEntry, final, finalProgress)), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1398	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, logoEyes, phoneLandscapeWelcomeBounds(lerpBounds(eyesEntry, final, finalProgress)), stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1399	    }
  1400	
  1401	    private fun drawPortraitProfile(
  1402	        canvas: Canvas,
  1403	        stage: StageMetrics,
  1404	        yOffset: Float,
  1405	        alpha: Float
  1406	    ) {
  1407	        val slideDistance = portraitAvatarSize()
  1408	        val dragDirection = portraitProfileSwipeDirection()
  1409	        val dragProgress = portraitProfileDragProgress()
  1410	        val carouselProgress = portraitProfileCarouselProgress()
  1411	        val carouselDirection = portraitProfileCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  1412	
  1413	        if (portraitProfileSwipeMoved && dragDirection != null && !isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()) {
  1414	            val direction = dragDirection.stageDirection
  1415	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1416	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * (1f - dragProgress), portraitProfileIndex,
  1417	                -direction * dragProgress * slideDistance
  1418	            )
  1419	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1420	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * dragProgress, portraitProfileIndexForDirection(dragDirection),
  1421	                direction * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance
  1422	            )
  1423	        } else if (isPortraitProfileCarouselAnimating()) {
  1424	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1425	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * (1f - carouselProgress), portraitProfileCarouselFromIndex,
  1426	                -carouselDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance
  1427	            )
  1428	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(
  1429	                canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha * carouselProgress, portraitProfileCarouselToIndex,
  1430	                carouselDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance
  1431	            )
  1432	        } else {
  1433	            drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(canvas, stage, yOffset, alpha, portraitProfileIndex, 0f)
  1434	        }
  1435	    }

  1450	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinAvatar, phoneLandscapeWelcomeBounds(Bounds(390f, 450f + stackShiftY + yOffset, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE)), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1451	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyAvatar, phoneLandscapeWelcomeBounds(Bounds(628f, 454f + stackShiftY + yOffset, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE, SIGN_IN_AVATAR_SIZE)), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)

  1453

  1455	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, martinName, phoneLandscapeWelcomeBounds(Bounds(368f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + yOffset, 220f, 72f)), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)
  1456	        drawFigmaBitmap(canvas, jannyName, phoneLandscapeWelcomeBounds(Bounds(606f, SIGN_IN_NAME_TOP + stackShiftY + yOffset, 220f, 72f)), stage.left, stage.top, stage.scale, alpha)

  1461	    private fun drawPortraitProfileAtIndex(

  1491	                avatarSize
  1492	            ),

  1494	            stage.top,
  1495	            stage.scale,
  1496	            alpha
  1497	        )
  1498	        drawFigmaBitmap(
  1499	            canvas,
  1500	            nameBitmap,
  1501	            Bounds(
  1502	                (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - nameWidth) / 2f + offsetX,
  1503	                portraitProfileNameTop() + yOffset,
  1504	                nameWidth,
  1505	                portraitProfileNameHeight()
  1506	            ),
  1507	            stage.left,
  1508	            stage.top,
  1509	            stage.scale,
  1510	            alpha
  1511	        )
  1512	    }
  1513	
  1514	    private fun drawIntroBackground(canvas: Canvas, solidProgress: Float, gradientProgress: Float) {
  1515	        val standardSignInBackground = standardSignInBackgroundForSize()
  1516	        if (gradientProgress <= 0.001f) {
  1517	            canvas.drawColor(lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress))
  1518	            return
  1519	        }
  1520	
  1521	        val solid = lerpColor(COLOR_FRAME_1, COLOR_FRAME_2, solidProgress)
  1522	        backgroundPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  1523	            0f,
  1524	            0f,
  1525	            0f,
  1526	            height.toFloat(),
  1527	            intArrayOf(
  1528	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_TOP, gradientProgress),
  1529	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_CENTER, gradientProgress),
  1530	                lerpColor(solid, COLOR_GRADIENT_BOTTOM, gradientProgress)
  1531	            ),
  1532	            null,
  1533	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  1534	        )
  1535	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1536	        backgroundPaint.shader = null
  1537	
  1538	        if (standardSignInBackground != null) {
  1539	            drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas, gradientProgress)
  1540	            drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas, standardSignInBackground, gradientProgress)
  1541	        }
  1542	    }
  1543	
  1544	    private fun drawStandardSignInBlackMatte(canvas: Canvas, alpha: Float) {
  1545	        backgroundPaint.color = COLOR_FRAME_1
  1546	        backgroundPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1547	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), backgroundPaint)
  1548	        backgroundPaint.alpha = 255
  1549	    }
  1550	
  1551	    private fun drawStandardSignInBackground(canvas: Canvas, bitmap: Bitmap, alpha: Float) {
  1552	        tempRect.set(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat())
  1553	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * STANDARD_BACKGROUND_SETTLED_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  1554	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1555	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1556	    }
  1557	
  1558	    private fun drawFigmaBitmap(
  1559	        canvas: Canvas,
  1560	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  1561	        bounds: Bounds,
  1562	        stageLeft: Float,
  1563	        stageTop: Float,
  1564	        stageScale: Float,
  1565	        alpha: Float
  1566	    ) {
  1567	        tempRect.set(
  1568	            stageLeft + bounds.x * stageScale,
  1569	            stageTop + bounds.y * stageScale,
  1570	            stageLeft + (bounds.x + bounds.w) * stageScale,
  1571	            stageTop + (bounds.y + bounds.h) * stageScale
  1572	        )
  1573	        imagePaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1574	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempRect, imagePaint)
  1575	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  1576	    }

  1578	    private fun drawAccountPrompt(
  1579	        canvas: Canvas,
  1580	        stageLeft: Float,
  1581	        stageTop: Float,
  1582	        stageScale: Float,
  1583	        yOffset: Float,
  1584	        alpha: Float
  1585	    ) {
  1586	        if (usesStickyAuthChrome(activeMockFlow) ||
  1587	            usesStickyAuthChrome(outgoingMockFlow)
  1588	        ) {
  1589	            return
  1590	        }
  1591	        val isLandscape = width > height
  1592	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  1593	        val textSize = lerpFloat(
  1594	            signInAccountPromptTextSize(),
  1595	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * PORTRAIT_ACTION_SCALE,
  1596	            portraitAmount
  1597	        ) * phoneLandscapeWelcomeAssetScale() * stageScale
  1598	        val paintAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  1599	        accountPromptPaint.textSize = textSize
  1600	        formTitlePaint.textSize = textSize
  1601	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1602	        formTitlePaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  1603	
  1604	        val accountPromptShiftY = if (isLandscape) SIGN_IN_LANDSCAPE_ACCOUNT_PROMPT_EXTRA_SHIFT_Y else 0f
  1605	        val standardCenterY = stageTop + phoneLandscapeWelcomeY(
  1606	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + accountPromptShiftY + yOffset
  1607	        ) * stageScale
  1608	        val secondaryMetrics = accountPromptPaint.fontMetrics
  1609	        val primaryMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  1610	        fun promptCenterY(portraitCenterY: Float): Float = lerpFloat(
  1611	            standardCenterY,
  1612	            stageTop + (portraitCenterY + yOffset) * stageScale,
  1613	            portraitAmount
  1614	        )
  1615	        val createCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptCreateCenterY())
  1616	        val signInCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptSignInCenterY())
  1617	        val forgotCenterY = promptCenterY(portraitPromptForgotCenterY())
  1618	        val createBaselineY = createCenterY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1619	        val signInBaselineY = signInCenterY - (primaryMetrics.ascent + primaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1620	        val forgotBaselineY = forgotCenterY - (secondaryMetrics.ascent + secondaryMetrics.descent) / 2f
  1621	        val portraitCenterX = FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH / 2f
  1622	        if (
  1623	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  1624	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount
  1625	        ) {
  1626	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1627	            canvas.drawText(
  1628	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT,
  1629	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(accountPromptCreateCenterX(), portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1630	                createBaselineY,
  1631	                accountPromptPaint
  1632	            )
  1633	        }
  1634	        if (
  1635	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn &&
  1636	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn
  1637	        ) {
  1638	            formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1639	            canvas.drawText(
  1640	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  1641	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X, portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1642	                signInBaselineY,
  1643	                formTitlePaint
  1644	            )
  1645	        }
  1646	        if (
  1647	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  1648	            outgoingMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  1649	        ) {
  1650	            accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1651	            canvas.drawText(
  1652	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT,
  1653	                stageLeft + lerpFloat(accountPromptForgotCenterX(), portraitCenterX, portraitAmount) * stageScale,
  1654	                forgotBaselineY,
  1655	                accountPromptPaint
  1656	            )
  1657	        }
  1658	        accountPromptPaint.alpha = 255
  1659	        accountPromptPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  1660	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1661	    }

  1663	    private fun drawMockSignInForm(
  1664	        canvas: Canvas,
  1665	        stageLeft: Float,
  1666	        stageTop: Float,
  1667	        stageScale: Float,
  1668	        progress: Float,
  1669	        formFocusMotion: Float,
  1670	        updateLabelAnimation: Boolean = true
  1671	    ) {
  1672	        if (updateLabelAnimation) {
  1673	            updateMockFormLabelAnimation()
  1674	        }
  1675	
  1676	        val alpha = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  1677	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  1678	            drawMockCreateAccountForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1679	            return
  1680	        }
  1681	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  1682	            drawMockForgotPasswordForm(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha, formFocusMotion)
  1683	            return
  1684	        }
  1685	
  1686	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - alpha)
  1687	        val fieldFillAlpha = (alpha * 26f).roundToInt()
  1688	        val fieldStrokeAlpha = (alpha * 185f).roundToInt()
  1689	        val baseStage = StageMetrics(stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale)
  1690	        val usernameY = mockPrimaryFormY() + yOffset
  1691	        val passwordY = usernameY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + mockFormFieldGap()
  1692	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  1693	        val contentStage = mockSignInContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  1694	        val contentLeft = contentStage.left
  1695	        val contentTop = contentStage.top
  1696	        val contentScale = contentStage.scale
  1697	        val fieldX = mockFormX()
  1698	
  1699	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb(fieldFillAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1700	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(fieldStrokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  1701	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * contentScale
  1702	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * contentScale
  1703	
  1704	        val rememberMeY = passwordY + MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TOP_GAP + focusShiftY
  1705	        val titleFieldY = usernameY + if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) 0f else focusShiftY
  1706	        if (usesStickyAuthChrome()) {
  1707	            canvas.save()
  1708	            canvas.clipRect(
  1709	                0f,
  1710	                authTitleClipTopPx(baseStage),
  1711	                width.toFloat(),
  1712	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(baseStage)
  1713	            )
  1714	        }
  1715	        drawMockSignInTitle(canvas, titleFieldY, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  1716	        if (usesStickyAuthChrome()) {
  1717	            canvas.restore()
  1718	            canvas.save()
  1719	            canvas.clipRect(
  1720	                0f,
  1721	                portraitAuthViewportTopPx(baseStage),
  1722	                width.toFloat(),
  1723	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(baseStage)
  1724	            )
  1725	        }
  1726	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Username, fieldX, usernameY + focusShiftY, contentLeft, contentTop, contentScale, alpha)
  1727	        drawMockSignInField(canvas, MockSignInField.Password, fieldX, passwordY + focusShiftY, contentLeft, contentTop, contentScale, alpha)
  1728	        drawRememberMeCheckbox(
  1729	            canvas,
  1730	            fieldX,
  1731	            rememberMeY,
  1732	            contentLeft,
  1733	            contentTop,
  1734	            contentScale,
  1735	            alpha
  1736	        )
  1737	        drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  1738	            canvas,
  1739	            contentLeft,
  1740	            contentTop,
  1741	            contentScale,
  1742	            alpha,
  1743	            yOffset,
  1744	            focusShiftY
  1745	        )
  1746	        if (usesStickyAuthChrome()) {
  1747	            canvas.restore()
  1748	        }
  1749	
  1750	        formLabelPaint.alpha = 255
  1751	        formInputPaint.alpha = 255
  1752	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  1753	        formCaretPaint.alpha = 255
  1754	    }

  1756	    private fun drawMockCreateAccountForm(

  1766	        val fieldStrokeAlpha = (alpha * 185f).roundToInt()

  1769	        val contentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)

  1780	        val titleFieldY = firstFieldY - if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) focusShiftY else 0f

  2089	    private fun drawMockInputDimOverlayAndActiveControl(
  2090	        canvas: Canvas,
  2091	        stageLeft: Float,
  2092	        stageTop: Float,
  2093	        stageScale: Float,
  2094	        alpha: Float,
  2095	        yOffset: Float,
  2096	        focusShiftY: Float
  2097	    ) {
  2098	        val dimProgress = mockInputDimProgress.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  2099	        if (dimProgress <= 0.01f) return
  2100	
  2101	        inputDimPaint.alpha = (dimProgress * MOCK_INPUT_DIM_ALPHA * 255f).roundToInt()
  2102	        canvas.drawRect(0f, 0f, width.toFloat(), height.toFloat(), inputDimPaint)
  2103	        inputDimPaint.alpha = 255
  2104	
  2105	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 26f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2106	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 185f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2107	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2108	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = 1.45f * stageScale
  2109	
  2110	        val activeDropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: mockInputDimActiveDropdown
  2111	        if (activeDropdown != null && activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  2112	            drawMockDropdownControl(
  2113	                canvas,
  2114	                activeDropdown,
  2115	                mockDropdownX(activeDropdown),
  2116	                mockDropdownY(activeDropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  2117	                stageLeft,
  2118	                stageTop,
  2119	                stageScale,
  2120	                alpha,
  2121	                mockDropdownWidth(activeDropdown)
  2122	            )
  2123	            if (expandedMockDropdown == activeDropdown) {
  2124	                drawMockDropdownOptions(canvas, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale, alpha)
  2125	            }
  2126	            return
  2127	        }
  2128	
  2129	        val activeField = focusedMockField ?: mockInputDimActiveField ?: return
  2130	        drawMockSignInField(
  2131	            canvas,
  2132	            activeField,
  2133	            mockActiveFieldX(activeField),
  2134	            mockActiveFieldY(activeField, yOffset, focusShiftY),
  2135	            stageLeft,
  2136	            stageTop,
  2137	            stageScale,
  2138	            alpha,
  2139	            mockActiveFieldWidth(activeField)
  2140	        )
  2141	    }

  2144	        canvas: Canvas,

  2146	        stageLeft: Float,

  2148	        stageScale: Float,
  2149	        progress: Float,
  2150	        formFocusMotion: Float,
  2151	        updateLabelAnimation: Boolean
  2152	    ) {
  2153	        val currentFlow = activeMockFlow
  2154	        activeMockFlow = flow
  2155	        try {
  2156	            drawMockSignInForm(
  2157	                canvas,

  2159	                stageTop,
  2160	                stageScale,
  2161	                progress,

  2166	            activeMockFlow = currentFlow
  2167	        }
  2168	    }

  2170	    private fun drawMockSignInTitle(
  2171	        canvas: Canvas,
  2172	        fieldTopY: Float,
  2173	        stageLeft: Float,
  2174	        stageTop: Float,
  2175	        stageScale: Float,
  2176	        alpha: Float
  2177	    ) {
  2178	        val motion = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  2179	        val portrait = isPortraitIntroLayout()
  2180	        val portraitTextSize = ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * PORTRAIT_ACTION_SCALE * stageScale
  2181	        val sourceTextSize = if (portrait) portraitTextSize else signInAccountPromptTextSize() * stageScale
  2182	        val targetTextSize = if (portrait) portraitTextSize else MOCK_FORM_TITLE_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2183	        val sourceCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  2184	        val targetCenterX = stageLeft + ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_CENTER_X * stageScale
  2185	        val sourceCenterY = stageTop + (if (portrait) {
  2186	            portraitPromptSignInCenterY()
  2187	        } else {
  2188	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY()
  2189	        }) * stageScale
  2190	        formTitlePaint.textSize = sourceTextSize
  2191	        val sourceMetrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  2192	        val sourceBaselineY = sourceCenterY - (sourceMetrics.ascent + sourceMetrics.descent) / 2f
  2193	        val targetBaselineY = mockFormTitleTargetBaseline(
  2194	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  2195	            fieldTopY,
  2196	            stageTop,
  2197	            stageScale,
  2198	            targetTextSize
  2199	        )
  2200	
  2201	        formTitlePaint.textSize = lerpFloat(sourceTextSize, targetTextSize, motion)
  2202	        formTitlePaint.alpha = 255
  2203	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2204	        canvas.drawText(
  2205	            ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT,
  2206	            lerpFloat(sourceCenterX, targetCenterX, motion),
  2207	            lerpFloat(sourceBaselineY, targetBaselineY, motion),
  2208	            formTitlePaint
  2209	        )
  2210	    }

  2303	
  2304	    private fun mockFormTitleTargetBaseline(
  2305	        title: String,
  2306	        contentTopY: Float,
  2307	        stageTop: Float,
  2308	        stageScale: Float,
  2309	        targetTextSize: Float
  2310	    ): Float {
  2311	        formTitlePaint.textSize = targetTextSize
  2312	        val titlePath = Path()
  2313	        val titleBounds = RectF()
  2314	        formTitlePaint.getTextPath(title, 0, title.length, 0f, 0f, titlePath)
  2315	        titlePath.computeBounds(titleBounds, true)
  2316	        val contentScaleRatio = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) portraitCreateContentScale() else 1f
  2317	        return stageTop + contentTopY * stageScale -
  2318	            mockFormFieldGap() * 2f * stageScale * contentScaleRatio -
  2319	            MOCK_FORM_TITLE_TO_FIELDS_EXTRA_GAP * stageScale * contentScaleRatio -
  2320	            titleBounds.bottom +
  2321	            MOCK_FORM_TITLE_OPTICAL_BOTTOM_OFFSET * stageScale * contentScaleRatio
  2322	    }
  2323	
  2324	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButton(
  2325	        canvas: Canvas,
  2326	        x: Float,
  2327	        y: Float,
  2328	        stageLeft: Float,
  2329	        stageTop: Float,
  2330	        stageScale: Float,
  2331	        alpha: Float,
  2332	        buttonWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  2333	    ) {
  2334	        tempRect.set(
  2335	            stageLeft + x * stageScale,
  2336	            stageTop + y * stageScale,
  2337	            stageLeft + (x + buttonWidth) * stageScale,
  2338	            stageTop + (y + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_HEIGHT) * stageScale
  2339	        )
  2340	        val radius = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_RADIUS * stageScale
  2341	        val submissionState = forgotPasswordSubmissionState
  2342	        val enabled = mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  2343	        val pressed = pressedForgotPasswordSubmit && enabled
  2344	        val fillAlpha = when {
  2345	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 86f
  2346	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 72f
  2347	            pressed -> 92f
  2348	            enabled -> 68f
  2349	            else -> 18f
  2350	        }
  2351	        val strokeAlpha = when {
  2352	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 245f
  2353	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 228f
  2354	            pressed -> 238f
  2355	            enabled -> 210f
  2356	            else -> 118f
  2357	        }
  2358	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  2359	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * fillAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2360	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * strokeAlpha).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  2361	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2362	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2363	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2364	        if (submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading) {
  2365	            drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(canvas, tempRect, radius, alpha)
  2366	        }
  2367	
  2368	        formTitlePaint.textSize = MOCK_REMEMBER_ME_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2369	        formTitlePaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2370	        val metrics = formTitlePaint.fontMetrics
  2371	        val baselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  2372	        val maxTextWidth = buttonWidth * stageScale - MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * stageScale * 2f
  2373	        val showCheck = submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  2374	        val checkSize = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_SIZE * stageScale else 0f
  2375	        val checkGap = if (showCheck) MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SENT_CHECK_GAP * stageScale else 0f
  2376	        val textMaxWidth = (maxTextWidth - checkSize - checkGap).coerceAtLeast(maxTextWidth * 0.5f)
  2377	        val buttonText = trailingFittingText(
  2378	            mockForgotPasswordButtonText(),
  2379	            if (showCheck) textMaxWidth else maxTextWidth,
  2380	            formTitlePaint
  2381	        )
  2382	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * when {
  2383	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading -> 226f
  2384	            submissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent -> 255f
  2385	            enabled -> 255f
  2386	            else -> 138f
  2387	        }).roundToInt()
  2388	        formTitlePaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2389	        val textX = if (showCheck) {
  2390	            val textWidth = formTitlePaint.measureText(buttonText)
  2391	            val groupWidth = checkSize + checkGap + textWidth
  2392	            val groupLeft = tempRect.centerX() - groupWidth / 2f
  2393	            val checkLeft = groupLeft
  2394	            val checkCenterY = tempRect.centerY()
  2395	            backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  2396	            backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 2.1f * stageScale
  2397	            canvas.drawLine(
  2398	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.18f,
  2399	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.03f,
  2400	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  2401	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  2402	                backChevronPaint
  2403	            )
  2404	            canvas.drawLine(
  2405	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.42f,
  2406	                checkCenterY + checkSize * 0.26f,
  2407	                checkLeft + checkSize * 0.84f,
  2408	                checkCenterY - checkSize * 0.28f,
  2409	                backChevronPaint
  2410	            )
  2411	            groupLeft + checkSize + checkGap + textWidth / 2f
  2412	        } else {
  2413	            tempRect.centerX()
  2414	        }
  2415	        canvas.drawText(
  2416	            buttonText,
  2417	            textX,
  2418	            baselineY,
  2419	            formTitlePaint
  2420	        )
  2421	    }

  2509	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordButtonShimmer(
  2510	        canvas: Canvas,
  2511	        buttonRect: RectF,
  2512	        radius: Float,
  2513	        alpha: Float
  2514	    ) {
  2515	        val progress = mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress()
  2516	        val sweepWidth = buttonRect.width() * 0.44f
  2517	        val sweepCenterX = buttonRect.left + buttonRect.width() * (progress * 1.35f - 0.18f)
  2518	        val sweepLeft = sweepCenterX - sweepWidth / 2f
  2519	        val sweepRight = sweepCenterX + sweepWidth / 2f
  2520	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = LinearGradient(
  2521	            sweepLeft,
  2522	            buttonRect.top,
  2523	            sweepRight,
  2524	            buttonRect.top,
  2525	            intArrayOf(
  2526	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255),
  2527	                Color.argb((alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 54f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255),
  2528	                Color.argb(0, 255, 255, 255)
  2529	            ),
  2530	            floatArrayOf(0f, 0.5f, 1f),
  2531	            Shader.TileMode.CLAMP
  2532	        )
  2533	        canvas.save()
  2534	        tempPath.reset()
  2535	        tempPath.addRoundRect(buttonRect, radius, radius, Path.Direction.CW)
  2536	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  2537	        canvas.drawRect(sweepLeft, buttonRect.top, sweepRight, buttonRect.bottom, formFieldFillPaint)
  2538	        canvas.restore()
  2539	        formFieldFillPaint.shader = null
  2540	    }

  2599	    private fun drawMockForgotRecoveryOption(

  2612	        val pressed = pressedForgotRecoveryTarget == target

  2614	            pressed -> 82f

  2617	        }).roundToInt()
  2618	        val strokeAlpha = (alpha * when {
  2619	            pressed -> 245f

  2621	            else -> 142f
  2622	        }).roundToInt()

  2626	        formFieldStrokePaint.color = Color.argb(strokeAlpha, 255, 255, 255)
  2627	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  2628	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  2629	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  2630	
  2631	        formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_OPTION_TEXT_SIZE * stageScale
  2632	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (selected || pressed) 255f else 178f).roundToInt()
  2633	        formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.CENTER
  2634	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2635	        canvas.drawText(
  2636	            target.displayText,
  2637	            tempRect.centerX(),
  2638	            tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  2639	            formInputPaint
  2640	        )
  2641	    }
