  2643	    private fun drawMockForgotPasswordBodyParagraph(
  2644	        canvas: Canvas,
  2645	        text: String,
  2646	        centerX: Float,
  2647	        topY: Float,
  2648	        maxWidth: Float,
  2649	        stageLeft: Float,
  2650	        stageTop: Float,
  2651	        stageScale: Float,
  2652	        alpha: Float
  2653	    ): Float {
  2654	        val paragraphs = text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n"))
  2655	        val leadBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  2656	            canvas,
  2657	            paragraphs.firstOrNull().orEmpty(),
  2658	            centerX,
  2659	            topY,
  2660	            maxWidth,
  2661	            stageLeft,
  2662	            stageTop,
  2663	            stageScale,
  2664	            alpha,
  2665	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_TEXT_SIZE,
  2666	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2667	            forceLineBreaks = true
  2668	        )
  2669	        val middleText = paragraphs.getOrNull(1) ?: return leadBottomY
  2670	        val middleBottomY = drawMockFormParagraph(
  2671	            canvas,
  2672	            middleText,
  2673	            centerX,
  2674	            leadBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2675	            maxWidth,
  2676	            stageLeft,
  2677	            stageTop,
  2678	            stageScale,
  2679	            alpha,
  2680	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  2681	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2682	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  2683	        )
  2684	        val bottomText = paragraphs.drop(2).joinToString("\n\n")
  2685	        if (bottomText.isEmpty()) return middleBottomY
  2686	        return drawMockFormParagraph(
  2687	            canvas,
  2688	            bottomText,
  2689	            centerX,
  2690	            middleBottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2691	            maxWidth,
  2692	            stageLeft,
  2693	            stageTop,
  2694	            stageScale,
  2695	            alpha,
  2696	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  2697	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT,
  2698	            Paint.Align.LEFT
  2699	        )
  2700	    }
  2701	
  2702	    private fun drawMockFormParagraph(
  2703	        canvas: Canvas,
  2704	        text: String,
  2705	        centerX: Float,
  2706	        topY: Float,
  2707	        maxWidth: Float,
  2708	        stageLeft: Float,
  2709	        stageTop: Float,
  2710	        stageScale: Float,
  2711	        alpha: Float,
  2712	        textSize: Float,
  2713	        lineHeight: Float,
  2714	        textAlign: Paint.Align = Paint.Align.CENTER,
  2715	        forceLineBreaks: Boolean = false
  2716	    ): Float {
  2717	        formInputPaint.textSize = textSize * stageScale
  2718	        formInputPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 218f).roundToInt()
  2719	        formInputPaint.textAlign = textAlign
  2720	
  2721	        val lines = if (forceLineBreaks) {
  2722	            text.split('\n').flatMap { line ->
  2723	                wrappedTextLines(line, maxWidth * stageScale, formInputPaint)
  2724	            }
  2725	        } else {
  2726	            wrappedTextLines(text, maxWidth * stageScale, formInputPaint)
  2727	        }
  2728	        val metrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  2729	        val x = when (textAlign) {
  2730	            Paint.Align.LEFT -> stageLeft + (centerX - maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  2731	            Paint.Align.RIGHT -> stageLeft + (centerX + maxWidth / 2f) * stageScale
  2732	            Paint.Align.CENTER -> stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  2733	        }
  2734	        var baselineY = stageTop + topY * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  2735	        lines.forEach { line ->
  2736	            canvas.drawText(line, x, baselineY, formInputPaint)
  2737	            baselineY += lineHeight * stageScale
  2738	        }
  2739	        return topY + lines.size * lineHeight
  2740	    }

  2742	    private fun drawMockCreateAccountAvatarText(
  2743	        canvas: Canvas,
  2744	        columnX: Float,
  2745	        firstFieldY: Float,
  2746	        stageLeft: Float,
  2747	        stageTop: Float,
  2748	        stageScale: Float,
  2749	        alpha: Float
  2750	    ) {
  2751	        val centerX = stageLeft + (columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f) * stageScale
  2752	        val avatarBounds = mockCreateAvatarBounds(columnX, firstFieldY)
  2753	        val usesSeparatedMotionLayers = isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating() ||
  2754	            (activeMockCreateAvatarDrag && mockCreateAvatarDragMoved)
  2755	        if (usesSeparatedMotionLayers) {
  2756	            canvas.save()
  2757	            clipMockCreateAvatarCarousel(canvas, avatarBounds, stageLeft, stageTop, stageScale)
  2758	            drawMockCreateAvatarMotionLayer(
  2759	                canvas = canvas,
  2760	                bitmaps = createAvatarBitmaps,
  2761	                bounds = avatarBounds,
  2762	                stageLeft = stageLeft,
  2763	                stageTop = stageTop,
  2764	                stageScale = stageScale,
  2765	                alpha = alpha
  2766	            )
  2767	            canvas.restore()
  2768	        } else {
  2769	            drawFigmaBitmap(
  2770	                canvas,

  2772	                avatarBounds,
  2773	                stageLeft,
  2774	                stageTop,
  2775	                stageScale,
  2776	                alpha
  2777	            )
  2778	        }
  2779	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2780	            canvas,
  2781	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous,
  2782	            avatarBounds,
  2783	            stageLeft,
  2784	            stageTop,
  2785	            stageScale,
  2786	            alpha
  2787	        )
  2788	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  2789	            canvas,
  2790	            MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next,
  2791	            avatarBounds,
  2792	            stageLeft,
  2793	            stageTop,
  2794	            stageScale,
  2795	            alpha
  2796	        )
  2797	        val usernameBaselineY = stageTop + mockCreateUsernameBaselineY(firstFieldY) * stageScale
  2798	        drawMockCreateUsername(canvas, centerX, usernameBaselineY, stageScale, alpha)
  2799	    }
  2800	
  2801	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarTransitionOverlay(
  2802	        canvas: Canvas,
  2803	        stage: StageMetrics,
  2804	        mockMotion: Float,
  2805	        formFocusMotion: Float
  2806	    ) {
  2807	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) return
  2808	
  2809	        if (isMockCreateWelcomeTransition()) {
  2810	            drawMockCreateWelcomeTransition(
  2811	                canvas,
  2812	                stage,
  2813	                mockMotion.coerceIn(0f, 1f),
  2814	                formFocusMotion
  2815	            )
  2816	            return
  2817	        }
  2818	
  2819	        val outgoingFlow = outgoingMockFlow
  2820	        val transitionProgress = when {
  2821	            outgoingFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount -> {
  2822	                1f - FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFlowSwitchProgress())
  2823	            }
  2824	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount && outgoingFlow != null -> {
  2825	                FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFlowSwitchProgress())
  2826	            }
  2827	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount && isMockSignInAnimating() -> mockMotion
  2828	            else -> return
  2829	        }.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  2830	
  2831	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - transitionProgress)
  2832	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  2833	        val contentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  2834	
  2835	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2836	            canvas.save()
  2837	            canvas.clipRect(
  2838	                0f,
  2839	                portraitAuthViewportTopPx(stage),
  2840	                width.toFloat(),
  2841	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)
  2842	            )
  2843	        }
  2844	        drawMockCreateAccountAvatarText(
  2845	            canvas,
  2846	            mockCreateAvatarColumnX(),
  2847	            mockCreateAvatarSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY),
  2848	            contentStage.left,
  2849	            contentStage.top,
  2850	            contentStage.scale,
  2851	            1f
  2852	        )
  2853	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2854	            canvas.restore()
  2855	        }
  2856	    }
  2857	
  2858	    private fun isMockCreateWelcomeTransition(): Boolean {
  2859	        return isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  2860	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  2861	            outgoingMockFlow == null &&
  2862	            isMockSignInAnimating()
  2863	    }
  2864	
  2865	    private fun drawMockCreateWelcomeTransition(
  2866	        canvas: Canvas,
  2867	        stage: StageMetrics,
  2868	        motion: Float,
  2869	        formFocusMotion: Float
  2870	    ) {
  2871	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(formFocusMotion)
  2872	        val accessoryYOffset = MOCK_SIGN_IN_STACK_EXIT_Y * (1f - motion)
  2873	        val contentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(stage, accessoryYOffset, focusShiftY)
  2874	        val columnX = mockCreateAvatarColumnX()
  2875	        val sectionY = mockCreateAvatarSectionY(accessoryYOffset, focusShiftY)
  2876	        val settledSectionY = mockCreateAvatarSectionY(0f, 0f)
  2877	        val settledContentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(stage, 0f, 0f)
  2878	        val settledAvatarBounds = mockCreateAvatarBounds(columnX, settledSectionY)
  2879	
  2880	        drawWelcomeAvatarRelatedAssetsForCreateTransition(canvas, stage, motion)
  2881	
  2882	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  2883	            canvas.save()
  2884	            canvas.clipRect(
  2885	                0f,
  2886	                portraitAuthViewportTopPx(stage),
  2887	                width.toFloat(),
  2888	                portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)
  2889	            )
  2890	        }
  2891	        drawMockCreateUsername(
  2892	            canvas,
  2893	            contentStage.left + (columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f) * contentStage.scale,
  2894	            contentStage.top + mockCreateUsernameBaselineY(sectionY) * contentStage.scale,
  2895	            contentStage.scale,
  2896	            1f
  2897	        )
  2898	        drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(

  2910	                motion
  2911	            )
  2912	        )

  2916	            settledAvatarBounds,

  2918	            settledContentStage.top,

  2926	            )

  2928	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {

  2930	        }
  2931	
  2932	        val destinationBounds = Bounds(
  2933	            (settledContentStage.left + settledAvatarBounds.x * settledContentStage.scale - stage.left) /
  2934	                stage.scale,

  2936	                stage.scale,

  2943

  2945	            canvas,

  2947	            movingBounds,

  2950	            stage.scale,
  2951	            1f
  2952	        )
  2953	    }

  2955	    private fun mockCreateTransitionChevronCenterX(
  2956	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  2957	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  2958	        contentStage: StageMetrics,
  2959	        motion: Float
  2960	    ): Float {
  2961	        val settledCenterX = contentStage.left +
  2962	            mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * contentStage.scale
  2963	        val halfChevronWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_WIDTH * contentStage.scale / 2f
  2964	        val edgeCenterX = if (direction == MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous) {
  2965	            -halfChevronWidth
  2966	        } else {
  2967	            width.toFloat() + halfChevronWidth
  2968	        }
  2969	        return lerpFloat(edgeCenterX, settledCenterX, motion.coerceIn(0f, 1f))
  2970	    }

  3075	    private fun drawMockCreateUsername(
  3076	        canvas: Canvas,
  3077	        centerX: Float,
  3078	        baselineY: Float,
  3079	        stageScale: Float,
  3080	        alpha: Float
  3081	    ) {
  3082	        val showPlaceholder =
  3083	            !isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  3084	                usernameText.isEmpty() &&
  3085	                focusedMockField != MockSignInField.Username
  3086	        if (usernameText.isEmpty() && !showPlaceholder) return
  3087	
  3088	        val displayText = if (showPlaceholder) {
  3089	            MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_PLACEHOLDER_TEXT
  3090	        } else {
  3091	            usernameText
  3092	        }
  3093	        val displayAlpha = if (showPlaceholder) {
  3094	            alpha * MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_PLACEHOLDER_ALPHA
  3095	        } else {
  3096	            alpha
  3097	        }
  3098	
  3099	        val landscapeScale = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  3100	            1f
  3101	        } else {
  3102	            mockCreateLandscapeFirstImpressionScale()
  3103	        }
  3104	        val maxTextWidth =
  3105	            (MOCK_FORM_WIDTH - MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_MAX_WIDTH_INSET * 2f) *
  3106	                landscapeScale * stageScale
  3107	        val preferredTextSize = mockCreateUsernameTextSize() * stageScale
  3108	        createUsernameFillPaint.textSize = preferredTextSize
  3109	        val preferredTextWidth = createUsernameFillPaint.measureText(displayText)
  3110	        val fittedTextSize = if (preferredTextWidth > maxTextWidth && preferredTextWidth > 0f) {
  3111	            preferredTextSize * maxTextWidth / preferredTextWidth
  3112	        } else {
  3113	            preferredTextSize
  3114	        }
  3115	        val paintAlpha = (displayAlpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  3116	
  3117	        createUsernameStrokePaint.textSize = fittedTextSize
  3118	        createUsernameStrokePaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_STROKE_WIDTH * stageScale
  3119	        createUsernameStrokePaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  3120	        createUsernameStrokePaint.setShadowLayer(
  3121	            MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_SHADOW_RADIUS * stageScale,
  3122	            0f,
  3123	            MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_SHADOW_OFFSET_Y * stageScale,
  3124	            Color.argb((paintAlpha * 0.25f).roundToInt(), 0, 0, 0)
  3125	        )
  3126	        canvas.drawText(displayText, centerX, baselineY, createUsernameStrokePaint)
  3127	        createUsernameStrokePaint.clearShadowLayer()
  3128	
  3129	        createUsernameFillPaint.textSize = fittedTextSize
  3130	        createUsernameFillPaint.alpha = paintAlpha
  3131	        canvas.drawText(displayText, centerX, baselineY, createUsernameFillPaint)
  3132	
  3133	        createUsernameStrokePaint.alpha = 255
  3134	        createUsernameFillPaint.alpha = 255
  3135	    }

  3165	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarMotionLayer(
  3166	        canvas: Canvas,
  3167	        bitmaps: List<Bitmap>,
  3168	        bounds: Bounds,
  3169	        stageLeft: Float,
  3170	        stageTop: Float,
  3171	        stageScale: Float,
  3172	        alpha: Float,
  3173	        transitionProgressOverride: Float? = null
  3174	    ) {
  3175	        val carouselProgress = transitionProgressOverride ?: mockCreateAvatarCarouselProgress()
  3176	        val slideDirection = mockCreateAvatarCarouselDirection.stageDirection
  3177	        val slideDistance = mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(bounds.w)
  3178	        val dragDirection = mockCreateAvatarDragDirection()
  3179	        val dragProgress = transitionProgressOverride ?:
  3180	            mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(slideDistance, stageScale)
  3181	        val isDraggingAvatar = activeMockCreateAvatarDrag &&
  3182	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  3183	            dragDirection != null &&
  3184	            !isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()
  3185	
  3186	        fun draw(index: Int, offsetX: Float) {
  3187	            drawFigmaBitmap(
  3188	                canvas,
  3189	                bitmaps[index.coerceIn(0, MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT - 1)],
  3190	                Bounds(bounds.x + offsetX, bounds.y, bounds.w, bounds.h),
  3191	                stageLeft,
  3192	                stageTop,
  3193	                stageScale,
  3194	                alpha
  3195	            )
  3196	        }
  3197	
  3198	        if (isDraggingAvatar && dragDirection != null) {
  3199	            val direction = dragDirection.stageDirection
  3200	            draw(mockCreateAvatarIndex, -direction * dragProgress * slideDistance)
  3201	            draw(mockCreateAvatarIndexForDirection(dragDirection), direction * (1f - dragProgress) * slideDistance)
  3202	        } else if (isMockCreateAvatarCarouselAnimating()) {
  3203	            draw(mockCreateAvatarCarouselFromIndex, -slideDirection * carouselProgress * slideDistance)
  3204	            draw(mockCreateAvatarCarouselToIndex, slideDirection * (1f - carouselProgress) * slideDistance)
  3205	        } else {
  3206	            draw(mockCreateAvatarIndex, 0f)
  3207	        }
  3208	    }

  3210	    private fun clipMockCreateAvatarCarousel(
  3211	        canvas: Canvas,
  3212	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  3213	        stageLeft: Float,
  3214	        stageTop: Float,
  3215	        stageScale: Float
  3216	    ) {
  3217	        tempPath.reset()
  3218	        tempPath.addCircle(
  3219	            stageLeft + (avatarBounds.x + avatarBounds.w / 2f) * stageScale,
  3220	            stageTop + (avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h / 2f) * stageScale,
  3221	            avatarBounds.w * 0.5f * stageScale,
  3222	            Path.Direction.CW
  3223	        )
  3224	        canvas.clipPath(tempPath)
  3225	    }

  3227	    private fun drawMockCreateAvatarChevron(
  3228	        canvas: Canvas,
  3229	        direction: MockAvatarCarouselDirection,
  3230	        avatarBounds: Bounds,
  3231	        stageLeft: Float,
  3232	        stageTop: Float,
  3233	        stageScale: Float,
  3234	        alpha: Float,
  3235	        centerXOverridePx: Float? = null
  3236	    ) {
  3237	        val visualScale = mockCreateFirstImpressionScale()
  3238	        val centerX = centerXOverridePx ?:
  3239	            stageLeft + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterX(direction, avatarBounds) * stageScale
  3240	        val centerY = stageTop + mockCreateAvatarChevronCenterY(avatarBounds) * stageScale
  3241	        val halfWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_WIDTH * visualScale * stageScale / 2f
  3242	        val halfHeight = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * visualScale * stageScale / 2f
  3243	        val pressedAlpha = if (pressedMockCreateAvatarNav == direction) 0.62f else 0.9f
  3244	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * pressedAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  3245	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH *
  3246	            visualScale * stageScale
  3247	
  3248	        if (direction == MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous) {
  3249	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX - halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  3250	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY, centerX + halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  3251	        } else {
  3252	            canvas.drawLine(centerX - halfWidth, centerY - halfHeight, centerX + halfWidth, centerY, backChevronPaint)
  3253	            canvas.drawLine(centerX + halfWidth, centerY, centerX - halfWidth, centerY + halfHeight, backChevronPaint)
  3254	        }
  3255	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  3256	    }

  3258	    private fun drawMockDropdownControl(
  3259	        canvas: Canvas,

  3264	        stageTop: Float,
  3265	        stageScale: Float,
  3266	        alpha: Float,

  3325	                    (formInputPaint.fontMetrics.ascent + formInputPaint.fontMetrics.descent) / 2f,
  3326	                formInputPaint
  3327	            )
  3328	        }
  3329	
  3330	        val centerX = tempRect.right - chevronInset
  3331	        val centerY = tempRect.centerY() + 2f * stageScale
  3332	        val halfSize = mockDropdownChevronSize(dropdown) * stageScale / 2f
  3333	        backChevronPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  3334	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth = 1.65f * stageScale

  3407	            canvas.drawText(
  3408	                trailingFittingText(option, maxTextWidth, formInputPaint),
  3409	                left + optionTextInset + iconLaneWidth,
  3410	                (optionTop + optionBottom) / 2f - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f,
  3411	                formInputPaint
  3412	            )
  3413	        }
  3414	
  3415	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  3416	            drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  3417	                canvas,
  3418	                dropdown,
  3419	                top,
  3420	                right,
  3421	                optionHeight * visibleOptions.size,
  3422	                stageScale,
  3423	                optionAlpha
  3424	            )
  3425	        }
  3426	    }

  3428	    private fun drawMembershipTierIcon(
  3429	        canvas: Canvas,
  3430	        bitmap: Bitmap,
  3431	        left: Float,
  3432	        centerY: Float,
  3433	        iconSize: Float,
  3434	        alpha: Int
  3435	    ) {
  3436	        val bitmapAspect = bitmap.width.toFloat() / bitmap.height.toFloat()
  3437	        val width: Float
  3438	        val height: Float
  3439	        if (bitmapAspect >= 1f) {
  3440	            width = iconSize
  3441	            height = iconSize / bitmapAspect
  3442	        } else {
  3443	            width = iconSize * bitmapAspect
  3444	            height = iconSize
  3445	        }
  3446	        val top = centerY - height / 2f
  3447	        tempIconRect.set(left, top, left + width, top + height)
  3448	        imagePaint.alpha = alpha.coerceIn(0, 255)
  3449	        canvas.drawBitmap(bitmap, null, tempIconRect, imagePaint)
  3450	        imagePaint.alpha = 255
  3451	    }

  3593	    private fun drawMockDropdownScrollbar(
  3594	        canvas: Canvas,
  3595	        dropdown: MockDropdown,
  3596	        top: Float,
  3597	        right: Float,
  3598	        dropdownHeight: Float,
  3599	        stageScale: Float,
  3600	        alpha: Int
  3601	    ) {
  3602	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  3603	        val visibleCount = mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  3604	        val maxOffset = mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown)
  3605	        if (options.isEmpty() || maxOffset <= 0) return
  3606	
  3607	        val trackRight = right - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * stageScale
  3608	        val trackLeft = trackRight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale
  3609	        val trackTop = top + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  3610	        val trackBottom = top + dropdownHeight - MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_Y * stageScale
  3611	        val trackHeight = (trackBottom - trackTop).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  3612	        val thumbHeight = maxOf(14f * stageScale, trackHeight * visibleCount / options.size)
  3613	            .coerceAtMost(trackHeight)
  3614	        val thumbProgress = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown) / maxOffset.toFloat()
  3615	        val thumbTop = trackTop + (trackHeight - thumbHeight) * thumbProgress
  3616	        val radius = MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH * stageScale / 2f
  3617	
  3618	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, trackTop, trackRight, trackBottom)
  3619	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.28f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  3620	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  3621	
  3622	        tempRect.set(trackLeft, thumbTop, trackRight, thumbTop + thumbHeight)
  3623	        formFieldFillPaint.color = Color.argb((alpha * 0.78f).roundToInt(), 255, 255, 255)
  3624	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  3625	    }

  3627	    private fun drawMockSignInField(
  3628	        canvas: Canvas,
  3629	        field: MockSignInField,
  3630	        x: Float,
  3631	        y: Float,
  3632	        stageLeft: Float,
  3633	        stageTop: Float,
  3634	        stageScale: Float,
  3635	        alpha: Float,
  3636	        fieldWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  3637	    ) {
  3638	        val visualScale = mockCreateLandscapeFieldVisualScale(field)
  3639	        val visualWidth = fieldWidth * visualScale
  3640	        val visualHeight = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT * visualScale
  3641	        val visualX = x - (visualWidth - fieldWidth) / 2f
  3642	        val visualY = y - (visualHeight - MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) / 2f
  3643	        val originalFieldStrokeWidth = formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth
  3644	        val originalCaretStrokeWidth = formCaretPaint.strokeWidth
  3645	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = originalFieldStrokeWidth * visualScale
  3646	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = originalCaretStrokeWidth * visualScale
  3647	        tempRect.set(
  3648	            stageLeft + visualX * stageScale,
  3649	            stageTop + visualY * stageScale,
  3650	            stageLeft + (visualX + visualWidth) * stageScale,
  3651	            stageTop + (visualY + visualHeight) * stageScale
  3652	        )
  3653	        val radius = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_RADIUS * visualScale * stageScale
  3654	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldFillPaint)
  3655	        canvas.drawRoundRect(tempRect, radius, radius, formFieldStrokePaint)
  3656	
  3657	        val label = mockSignInFieldLabel(field)
  3658	        val labelProgress = mockSignInFieldLabelProgress(field)
  3659	        val textAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * 255f).roundToInt()
  3660	        formLabelPaint.textSize = lerpFloat(
  3661	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE,
  3662	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * MOCK_FORM_FLOATING_LABEL_SCALE,
  3663	            labelProgress
  3664	        ) * visualScale * stageScale
  3665	        formLabelPaint.alpha = textAlpha
  3666	        formLabelPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  3667	        val metrics = formLabelPaint.fontMetrics
  3668	        val placeholderBaselineY = tempRect.centerY() - (metrics.ascent + metrics.descent) / 2f
  3669	        val floatingBaselineY = tempRect.top +
  3670	            MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_TOP_INSET_Y * visualScale * stageScale - metrics.ascent
  3671	        val labelX = lerpFloat(
  3672	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * visualScale * stageScale,
  3673	            tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_FLOAT_INSET_X * visualScale * stageScale,
  3674	            labelProgress
  3675	        )
  3676	        val labelBaselineY = lerpFloat(placeholderBaselineY, floatingBaselineY, labelProgress)
  3677	        canvas.drawText(
  3678	            label,
  3679	            labelX,
  3680	            labelBaselineY,
  3681	            formLabelPaint
  3682	        )
  3683	
  3684	        val fieldText = mockSignInFieldDisplayText(field)
  3685	        val shouldDrawInput = fieldText.isNotEmpty() || focusedMockField == field
  3686	        if (shouldDrawInput) {
  3687	            val inputAlpha = (alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * labelProgress * 255f).roundToInt()
  3688	            formInputPaint.textSize = MOCK_FORM_LABEL_TEXT_SIZE * visualScale * stageScale
  3689	            formInputPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  3690	            formInputPaint.textAlign = Paint.Align.LEFT
  3691	            formCaretPaint.alpha = inputAlpha
  3692	
  3693	            val inputX = tempRect.left + MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * visualScale * stageScale
  3694	            val inputBaselineY = tempRect.top +
  3695	                MOCK_FORM_INPUT_BASELINE_FROM_TOP * visualScale * stageScale
  3696	            val maxInputWidth = visualWidth * stageScale -
  3697	                MOCK_FORM_LABEL_INSET_X * visualScale * stageScale * 2f
  3698	            val visibleText = trailingFittingText(fieldText, maxInputWidth, formInputPaint)
  3699	            canvas.drawText(visibleText, inputX, inputBaselineY, formInputPaint)
  3700	
  3701	            if (focusedMockField == field) {
  3702	                val caretX = inputX + formInputPaint.measureText(visibleText) +
  3703	                    2f * visualScale * stageScale
  3704	                val inputMetrics = formInputPaint.fontMetrics
  3705	                canvas.drawLine(
  3706	                    caretX,
  3707	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.ascent,
  3708	                    caretX,
  3709	                    inputBaselineY + inputMetrics.descent,
  3710	                    formCaretPaint
  3711	                )
  3712	            }
  3713	        }
  3714	        formFieldStrokePaint.strokeWidth = originalFieldStrokeWidth
  3715	        formCaretPaint.strokeWidth = originalCaretStrokeWidth
  3716	    }

  3777	    private fun drawBackChevron(
  3778	        canvas: Canvas,
  3779	        centerX: Float,
  3780	        topY: Float,
  3781	        stageLeft: Float,
  3782	        stageTop: Float,
  3783	        stageScale: Float,
  3784	        alpha: Float
  3785	    ) {
  3786	        val chevronAlpha = alpha.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * if (pressedBackChevron) 0.68f else 0.92f
  3787	        val chevronScale = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  3788	            MOCK_PORTRAIT_STICKY_BOTTOM_CHEVRON_SCALE
  3789	        } else {
  3790	            1f
  3791	        }
  3792	        val center = stageLeft + centerX * stageScale
  3793	        val top = stageTop + topY * stageScale
  3794	        val bottom = top + MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * chevronScale * stageScale
  3795	        val halfWidth = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_WIDTH * chevronScale * stageScale / 2f
  3796	
  3797	        backChevronPaint.alpha = (chevronAlpha * 255f).roundToInt()
  3798	        val strokeScale = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  3799	            MOCK_PORTRAIT_STICKY_BOTTOM_CHEVRON_STROKE_SCALE
  3800	        } else {
  3801	            1f
  3802	        }
  3803	        backChevronPaint.strokeWidth =
  3804	            MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_STROKE_WIDTH * chevronScale * strokeScale * stageScale
  3805	        canvas.drawLine(center - halfWidth, top, center, bottom, backChevronPaint)
  3806	        canvas.drawLine(center, bottom, center + halfWidth, top, backChevronPaint)
  3807	        backChevronPaint.alpha = 255
  3808	    }

  3829	    private fun decode(resId: Int): Bitmap {

  3832	
  3833	    private data class CreateAvatarLayers(
  3834	        val bubble: Bitmap,
  3835	        val characters: List<Bitmap>,
  3836	        val bottomStrokes: List<Bitmap>
  3837	    )
  3838	
  3839	    private fun createAvatarCarouselLayers(vararg avatars: Bitmap): CreateAvatarLayers {
  3840	        require(avatars.size == MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_COUNT)
  3841	        val bitmapWidth = avatars.first().width
  3842	        val bitmapHeight = avatars.first().height
  3843	        require(avatars.all { it.width == bitmapWidth && it.height == bitmapHeight })
  3844	        return CreateAvatarLayers(
  3845	            bubble = createAvatarBubbleShellBitmap(avatars.first()),
  3846	            characters = avatars.map(::createAvatarCharacterBitmap),
  3847	            bottomStrokes = avatars.map(::createAvatarBottomStrokeBitmap)
  3848	        )
  3849	    }
  3850	
  3851	    private fun createAvatarBottomStrokeBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  3852	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  3853	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  3854	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  3855	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3856	        val strokePixels = IntArray(pixels.size)
  3857	        val minimumBodyY = (bitmapHeight * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BOTTOM_STROKE_START_RATIO).roundToInt()
  3858	
  3859	        for (x in 0 until bitmapWidth) {
  3860	            var bodyBottomY = -1
  3861	            for (y in minimumBodyY until bitmapHeight) {
  3862	                if (isAvatarCharacterFillPixel(pixels[y * bitmapWidth + x])) bodyBottomY = y
  3863	            }
  3864	            if (bodyBottomY < 0) continue
  3865	            val strokeBottomY = minOf(bitmapHeight - 1, bodyBottomY + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BOTTOM_STROKE_DEPTH_PX)
  3866	            for (y in bodyBottomY + 1..strokeBottomY) {
  3867	                val index = y * bitmapWidth + x
  3868	                val sourceAlpha = Color.alpha(pixels[index])
  3869	                if (sourceAlpha <= 16) continue
  3870	                strokePixels[index] = Color.argb(
  3871	                    sourceAlpha,
  3872	                    MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL,
  3873	                    MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL,
  3874	                    MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL
  3875	                )
  3876	            }
  3877	        }
  3878	        val cornerFilledStrokePixels = strokePixels.copyOf()
  3879	        for (y in minimumBodyY until bitmapHeight) {
  3880	            val rowStart = y * bitmapWidth
  3881	            for (x in 0 until bitmapWidth) {
  3882	                if (strokePixels[rowStart + x] == Color.TRANSPARENT) continue
  3883	                val sideStart = maxOf(0, x - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BOTTOM_STROKE_SIDE_EXTEND_PX)
  3884	                val sideEnd = minOf(bitmapWidth - 1, x + MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BOTTOM_STROKE_SIDE_EXTEND_PX)
  3885	                for (sideX in sideStart..sideEnd) {
  3886	                    val index = rowStart + sideX
  3887	                    val sourceAlpha = Color.alpha(pixels[index])
  3888	                    if (sourceAlpha <= 16 || isAvatarCharacterFillPixel(pixels[index])) continue
  3889	                    cornerFilledStrokePixels[index] = Color.argb(
  3890	                        sourceAlpha,
  3891	                        MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL,
  3892	                        MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL,
  3893	                        MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STROKE_CHANNEL
  3894	                    )
  3895	                }
  3896	            }
  3897	        }
  3898	        return bitmapFromPixels(cornerFilledStrokePixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3899	    }
  3900	
  3901	    private fun isAvatarCharacterFillPixel(pixel: Int): Boolean {
  3902	        if (Color.alpha(pixel) <= 16) return false
  3903	        val channelMax = maxOf(Color.red(pixel), Color.green(pixel), Color.blue(pixel))
  3904	        val channelMin = minOf(Color.red(pixel), Color.green(pixel), Color.blue(pixel))
  3905	        return channelMax > 40 && channelMax - channelMin > 8
  3906	    }
  3907	
  3908	    private fun createAvatarBubbleShellBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  3909	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  3910	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  3911	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  3912	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3913	
  3914	        val centerX = (bitmapWidth - 1f) / 2f
  3915	        val centerY = (bitmapHeight - 1f) / 2f
  3916	        val innerRadius = min(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight) *
  3917	            (0.5f - MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_BUBBLE_INNER_INSET_RATIO)
  3918	        val innerRadiusSquared = innerRadius * innerRadius
  3919	        val shellPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  3920	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  3921	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  3922	            val dx = x - centerX
  3923	            val dy = y - centerY
  3924	            if (dx * dx + dy * dy >= innerRadiusSquared) pixels[index] else Color.TRANSPARENT
  3925	        }
  3926	        return bitmapFromPixels(shellPixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3927	    }
  3928	
  3929	    private fun createAvatarCharacterBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  3930	        val bitmapWidth = source.width
  3931	        val bitmapHeight = source.height
  3932	        val pixels = IntArray(bitmapWidth * bitmapHeight)
  3933	        source.getPixels(pixels, 0, bitmapWidth, 0, 0, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3934	
  3935	        val centerX = (bitmapWidth - 1f) / 2f
  3936	        val centerY = (bitmapHeight - 1f) / 2f
  3937	        val innerRadius = min(bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight) * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CLIP_RADIUS_RATIO
  3938	        val innerRadiusSquared = innerRadius * innerRadius
  3939	        val distance = IntArray(pixels.size) { -1 }
  3940	        val queue = IntArray(pixels.size)
  3941	        var queueStart = 0
  3942	        var queueEnd = 0
  3943	
  3944	        pixels.indices.forEach { index ->
  3945	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  3946	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  3947	            val dx = x - centerX
  3948	            val dy = y - centerY
  3949	            if (dx * dx + dy * dy < innerRadiusSquared && isAvatarCharacterColor(pixels[index])) {
  3950	                distance[index] = 0
  3951	                queue[queueEnd++] = index
  3952	            }
  3953	        }
  3954	
  3955	        while (queueStart < queueEnd) {
  3956	            val index = queue[queueStart++]
  3957	            val currentDistance = distance[index]
  3958	            if (currentDistance >= MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_OUTLINE_RADIUS_PX) continue
  3959	            val x = index % bitmapWidth
  3960	            val y = index / bitmapWidth
  3961	            for (dy in -1..1) {
  3962	                val nextY = y + dy
  3963	                if (nextY !in 0 until bitmapHeight) continue
  3964	                for (dx in -1..1) {
  3965	                    if (dx == 0 && dy == 0) continue
  3966	                    val nextX = x + dx
  3967	                    if (nextX !in 0 until bitmapWidth) continue
  3968	                    val nextIndex = nextY * bitmapWidth + nextX
  3969	                    if (distance[nextIndex] >= 0 || !isAvatarOutlinePixel(pixels[nextIndex])) continue
  3970	                    val centerDx = nextX - centerX
  3971	                    val centerDy = nextY - centerY
  3972	                    if (centerDx * centerDx + centerDy * centerDy >= innerRadiusSquared) continue
  3973	                    distance[nextIndex] = currentDistance + 1
  3974	                    queue[queueEnd++] = nextIndex
  3975	                }
  3976	            }
  3977	        }
  3978	
  3979	        val characterPixels = IntArray(pixels.size) { index ->
  3980	            if (distance[index] >= 0) pixels[index] else Color.TRANSPARENT
  3981	        }
  3982	        return bitmapFromPixels(characterPixels, bitmapWidth, bitmapHeight)
  3983	    }
  3984	
  3985	    private fun isAvatarCharacterColor(pixel: Int): Boolean {
  3986	        if (Color.alpha(pixel) <= 16) return false
  3987	        val red = Color.red(pixel)
  3988	        val green = Color.green(pixel)
  3989	        val blue = Color.blue(pixel)
  3990	        val channelMax = maxOf(red, green, blue)
  3991	        val channelMin = minOf(red, green, blue)
  3992	        val isNeutralBubbleOrRing = channelMax - channelMin <= 4
  3993	        return (Color.alpha(pixel) >= 250 && !isNeutralBubbleOrRing) ||
  3994	            (channelMax > 40 && channelMax - channelMin > 8)
  3995	    }
  3996	
  3997	    private fun isAvatarOutlinePixel(pixel: Int): Boolean {
  3998	        return Color.alpha(pixel) > 24 && maxOf(Color.red(pixel), Color.green(pixel), Color.blue(pixel)) <= 80
  3999	    }
  4000	
  4001	    private fun bitmapFromPixels(pixels: IntArray, width: Int, height: Int): Bitmap {
  4002	        return Bitmap.createBitmap(width, height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888).apply {
  4003	            setPixels(pixels, 0, width, 0, 0, width, height)
  4004	        }
  4005	    }

  4007	    private fun standardSignInBackgroundForSize(): Bitmap? {
  4008	        return when {
  4009	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) &&
  4010	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) -> standardLandscapeBackground
  4011	            isSizeCloseTo(width, STANDARD_TABLET_SHORT_EDGE) &&
  4012	                isSizeCloseTo(height, STANDARD_TABLET_LONG_EDGE) -> standardPortraitBackground
  4013	            else -> null
  4014	        }
  4015	    }

  4017	    private fun isSizeCloseTo(actual: Int, expected: Int): Boolean {
  4018	        return abs(actual - expected) <= STANDARD_TABLET_SIZE_TOLERANCE
  4019	    }

  4026	    private fun settledAvatarHitProfile(x: Float, y: Float): CinerificProfile? {
  4027	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null) return null
  4028	        if (!isFinalFrameSettled()) return null
  4029	
  4030	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  4031	
  4032	        if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4033	            val avatarSize = portraitAvatarSize()
  4034	            val centerX = stage.left + FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  4035	            val halfWidth = (avatarSize / 2f + PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_HIT_HORIZONTAL_PADDING) * stage.scale
  4036	            val top = stage.top + portraitAvatarTop() * stage.scale
  4037	            val bottom = stage.top + (
  4038	                portraitProfileNameTop() + portraitProfileNameHeight() +
  4039	                    PORTRAIT_PROFILE_SWIPE_HIT_BOTTOM_PADDING
  4040	                ) * stage.scale
  4041	            return if (x in (centerX - halfWidth)..(centerX + halfWidth) && y in top..bottom) {
  4042	                FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS[portraitProfileIndex].profile
  4043	            } else {
  4044	                null
  4045	            }
  4046	        }
  4047	
  4048	        val stackShiftY = signInStackShiftY()
  4049	        return FINAL_AVATAR_TARGETS.firstOrNull { target ->
  4050	            val bounds = phoneLandscapeWelcomeBounds(
  4051	                Bounds(
  4052	                    target.bounds.x,
  4053	                    target.bounds.y + stackShiftY,
  4054	                    target.bounds.w,
  4055	                    target.bounds.h
  4056	                )
  4057	            )
  4058	            val centerX = stage.left + (bounds.x + bounds.w / 2f) * stage.scale
  4059	            val centerY = stage.top + (bounds.y + bounds.h / 2f) * stage.scale
  4060	            val radius = min(bounds.w, bounds.h) * stage.scale / 2f
  4061	            val dx = x - centerX
  4062	            val dy = y - centerY
  4063	            dx * dx + dy * dy <= radius * radius
  4064	        }?.profile
  4065	    }

  4328	
  4329	    private fun updateMockCreateAvatarDrag(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  4330	        if (!activeMockCreateAvatarDrag) return false
  4331	
  4332	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  4333	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockTouchDownY
  4334	        if (
  4335	            !mockCreateAvatarDragMoved &&
  4336	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > touchSlop &&
  4337	            abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) > abs(dragDeltaY)
  4338	        ) {
  4339	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = true
  4340	            lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = if (mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX < 0f) {
  4341	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Next
  4342	            } else {
  4343	                MockAvatarCarouselDirection.Previous
  4344	            }
  4345	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  4346	        }
  4347	
  4348	        if (mockCreateAvatarDragMoved) {
  4349	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  4350	        }
  4351	        return true
  4352	    }
  4353	
  4354	    private fun mockDropdownOptionHit(pointerX: Float, pointerY: Float): MockDropdownOption? {
  4355	        if (
  4356	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  4357	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount ||
  4358	            expandedMockDropdown == null
  4359	        ) {
  4360	            return null
  4361	        }
  4362	
  4363	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return null
  4364	        val baseStage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  4365	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  4366	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  4367	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  4368	        if (!portraitAuthPointerInViewport(pointerY, baseStage)) return null
  4369	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4370	        val dropdownX = mockDropdownX(dropdown)
  4371	        val yStart = mockDropdownOptionsY(dropdown, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4372	        val left = stage.left + dropdownX * stage.scale
  4373	        val right = stage.left + (dropdownX + mockDropdownWidth(dropdown)) * stage.scale
  4374	        if (pointerX !in left..right) return null
  4375	        if (mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  4376	            val scrollbarLeft = right -
  4377	                (MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_WIDTH + MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_SCROLLBAR_INSET_X * 2f) * stage.scale
  4378	            if (pointerX >= scrollbarLeft) return null
  4379	        }
  4380	
  4381	        mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown).forEachIndexed { optionIndex, option ->
  4382	            val optionHeight = mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown)
  4383	            val top = stage.top + (yStart + optionIndex * optionHeight) * stage.scale
  4384	            val bottom = top + optionHeight * stage.scale
  4385	            if (pointerY in top..bottom) return MockDropdownOption(dropdown, option)
  4386	        }
  4387	        return null
  4388	    }

  4390	    private fun mockDropdownOptionsHit(pointerX: Float, pointerY: Float): MockDropdown? {
  4391	        if (
  4392	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  4393	            activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount ||
  4394	            expandedMockDropdown == null
  4395	        ) {
  4396	            return null
  4397	        }
  4398	
  4399	        val dropdown = expandedMockDropdown ?: return null
  4400	        val baseStage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return null
  4401	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  4402	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  4403	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  4404	        if (!portraitAuthPointerInViewport(pointerY, baseStage)) return null
  4405	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4406	        val dropdownX = mockDropdownX(dropdown)

  4415	    private fun updateMockDropdownScroll(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  4416	        val dropdown = activeMockDropdownScroll ?: return false
  4417	        if (expandedMockDropdown != dropdown || !mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) return false
  4418	
  4419	        val baseStage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return true
  4420	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  4421	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  4422	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  4423	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4424	        val optionHeightPx = (mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown) * stage.scale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  4425	        val dragDeltaY = event.y - mockDropdownScrollStartY
  4426	        if (abs(dragDeltaY) > touchSlop) {
  4427	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = true
  4428	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  4429	        }
  4430	
  4431	        if (mockDropdownScrollMoved) {
  4432	            val rowDelta = ((mockDropdownScrollStartY - event.y) / optionHeightPx).roundToInt()
  4433	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, mockDropdownScrollStartOffset + rowDelta)
  4434	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  4435	        }
  4436	        return true
  4437	    }
  4438	
  4439	    private fun updatePortraitAuthScroll(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  4440	        if (
  4441	            !usesStickyAuthChrome() ||
  4442	            activeMockFlow !in listOf(
  4443	                MockAccountFlow.SignIn,
  4444	                MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount,
  4445	                MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword
  4446	            ) ||
  4447	            activeMockDropdownScroll != null
  4448	        ) {
  4449	            return false
  4450	        }
  4451	
  4452	        val deltaX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  4453	        val deltaY = event.y - portraitAuthScrollStartY
  4454	        if (!portraitAuthScrollMoved) {
  4455	            if (abs(deltaY) <= touchSlop || abs(deltaY) <= abs(deltaX)) return false
  4456	            if (isPortraitIntroLayout() && mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown && deltaY < 0f) {
  4457	                return false
  4458	            }
  4459	            mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown = false
  4460	            portraitAuthScrollMoved = true
  4461	            pressedMockField = null
  4462	            pressedMockDropdown = null
  4463	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  4464	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  4465	            activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  4466	            mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  4467	            expandedMockDropdown = null
  4468	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  4469	        }
  4470	
  4471	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return true
  4472	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  4473	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  4474	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  4475	        val maxScrollY = when (activeMockFlow) {
  4476	            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword -> portraitForgotPasswordMaxScrollY(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4477	            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount -> portraitCreateMaxScrollY(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4478	            else -> authSignInMaxScrollY(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4479	        }
  4480	        portraitAuthScrollY = (portraitAuthScrollStartOffset - deltaY).coerceIn(0f, maxScrollY)
  4481	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  4482	        return true
  4483	    }

  4485	    private fun mockBackChevronHit(x: Float, y: Float): Boolean {
  4486	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return false

  4499	    private fun shouldStartMockDragReturn(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
  4500	        if (
  4501	            !isPortraitIntroLayout() ||
  4502	            mockDragReturnInProgress ||
  4503	            isMockSignInClosing() ||
  4504	            !mockDragReturnEligibleAtTouchDown
  4505	        ) {
  4506	            return false
  4507	        }
  4508	
  4509	        val downwardScrollY = mockTouchDownY - event.y
  4510	        if (downwardScrollY <= mockBackDragThresholdPx()) return false
  4511	
  4512	        val dragX = event.x - mockTouchDownX
  4513	        return downwardScrollY > abs(dragX)
  4514	    }

  4516	    private fun isMockAuthScreenAtBottom(): Boolean {
  4517	        if (activeMockFlow == null) return false
  4518	
  4519	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return false
  4520	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  4521	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  4522	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(
  4523	            FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress)
  4524	        )
  4525	        val maxScrollY = when (activeMockFlow) {
  4526	            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount -> portraitCreateMaxScrollY(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4527	            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword -> portraitForgotPasswordMaxScrollY(
  4528	                stage,
  4529	                yOffset,
  4530	                focusShiftY
  4531	            )
  4532	            MockAccountFlow.SignIn -> authSignInMaxScrollY(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  4533	            null -> return false
  4534	        }
  4535	        return portraitAuthScrollY >= maxScrollY - maxOf(1f, stage.scale)
  4536	    }

  4538	    private fun mockBackDragThresholdPx(): Float {
  4539	        val stageThreshold = currentStageMetrics()?.let { stage ->
  4540	            MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD * stage.scale
  4541	        } ?: MOCK_BACK_DRAG_THRESHOLD
  4542	        return maxOf(stageThreshold, touchSlop * 3f)
  4543	    }

  4545	    private fun mockSignInFieldContains(
  4546	        pointerX: Float,
  4547	        pointerY: Float,
  4548	        fieldX: Float,
  4549	        fieldY: Float,
  4550	        stage: StageMetrics,
  4551	        fieldWidth: Float = MOCK_FORM_WIDTH,
  4552	        visualScale: Float = 1f,
  4553	        fieldHeight: Float = MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT
  4554	    ): Boolean {
  4555	        val visualWidth = fieldWidth * visualScale
  4556	        val visualHeight = fieldHeight * visualScale
  4557	        val visualX = fieldX - (visualWidth - fieldWidth) / 2f
  4558	        val visualY = fieldY - (visualHeight - fieldHeight) / 2f
  4559	        val left = stage.left + visualX * stage.scale
  4560	        val top = stage.top + visualY * stage.scale
  4561	        val right = stage.left + (visualX + visualWidth) * stage.scale
  4562	        val bottom = stage.top + (visualY + visualHeight) * stage.scale
  4563	        return pointerX in left..right && pointerY in top..bottom
  4564	    }

  4665	    private fun mockCreateAvatarTopY(firstFieldY: Float): Float {

  4679	    private fun mockCreateAvatarCarouselBounds(
  4680	        columnX: Float,
  4681	        firstFieldY: Float,
  4682	        avatarBounds: Bounds
  4683	    ): Bounds {
  4684	        val centerX = columnX + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f
  4685	        val contentTop = firstFieldY
  4686	        val contentBottom = avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h
  4687	        val contentCenterY = (contentTop + contentBottom) / 2f
  4688	        val chevronSpan = avatarBounds.w +
  4689	            (MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_SIDE_GAP +
  4690	                MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_CHEVRON_HIT_WIDTH / 2f) *
  4691	                mockCreateFirstImpressionScale() * 2f
  4692	        val side = maxOf(chevronSpan, contentBottom - contentTop)
  4693	        return Bounds(
  4694	            centerX - side / 2f,
  4695	            contentCenterY - side / 2f,
  4696	            side,
  4697	            side
  4698	        )
  4699	    }

  4738	    private fun focusMockSignInField(field: MockSignInField) {
  4739	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = field
  4740	        mockInputDimActiveField = field
  4741	        mockInputDimActiveDropdown = null
  4742	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  4743	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  4744	        if (focusedMockField != field) {
  4745	            activeComposingText = ""
  4746	            focusedMockField = field

  4748	            restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  4749	        }
  4750	        requestFocus()
  4751	        post {
  4752	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  4753	            inputMethodManager?.restartInput(this)
  4754	            inputMethodManager?.showSoftInput(this, InputMethodManager.SHOW_IMPLICIT)
  4755	        }
  4756	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  4757	    }

  4810	    private fun mockCreateUsernameBaselineY(firstFieldY: Float): Float {
  4811	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4812	            val avatarBounds = mockCreateAvatarBounds(mockCreateAvatarColumnX(), firstFieldY)
  4813	            val baseBaseline = avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h +
  4814	                MOCK_CREATE_LANDSCAPE_USERNAME_DISPLAY_GAP +
  4815	                MOCK_CREATE_LANDSCAPE_HERO_CENTER_SHIFT_UP
  4816	            createUsernameFillPaint.textSize = MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_TEXT_SIZE
  4817	            val originalCenterFromBaseline = createUsernameFillPaint.fontMetrics.let {
  4818	                (it.ascent + it.descent) / 2f
  4819	            }
  4820	            createUsernameFillPaint.textSize = mockCreateUsernameTextSize()
  4821	            val scaledCenterFromBaseline = createUsernameFillPaint.fontMetrics.let {
  4822	                (it.ascent + it.descent) / 2f
  4823	            }
  4824	            return baseBaseline + originalCenterFromBaseline - scaledCenterFromBaseline
  4825	        }
  4826	
  4827	        createUsernameFillPaint.textSize = MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_TEXT_SIZE
  4828	        val usernameTextTopFromBaseline = createUsernameFillPaint.fontMetrics.ascent
  4829	        val avatarBottomY = mockCreateAvatarTopY(firstFieldY) +
  4830	            MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE

  4832	    }

  4835	        createUsernameFillPaint.textSize = MOCK_CREATE_USERNAME_TEXT_SIZE
  4836	        val metrics = createUsernameFillPaint.fontMetrics
  4837	        val collapsedOffset = mockCreateAvatarTopY(0f) +
  4838	            MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE +
  4839	            MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_SECTION_GAP
  4840	        val expandedOffset = mockCreateUsernameBaselineY(0f) +
  4841	            metrics.descent +
  4842	            MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_SECTION_GAP

  4844	        return lerpFloat(collapsedOffset, expandedOffset, revealMotion)

  4952	    private fun mockSignInContentStageMetrics(
  4953	        stage: StageMetrics,
  4954	        yOffset: Float,
  4955	        focusShiftY: Float
  4956	    ): StageMetrics {
  4957	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.SignIn) return stage
  4958	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  4959	            return stage.copy(top = stage.top - portraitAuthScrollY)
  4960	        }
  4961	
  4962	        val contentScale = stage.scale * portraitCreateContentScale()
  4963	        val centerX = stage.left + FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stage.scale / 2f
  4964	        val firstFieldY = mockPrimaryFormY() + yOffset + focusShiftY
  4965	        val firstFieldTop = stage.top + firstFieldY * stage.scale
  4966	        return StageMetrics(
  4967	            left = centerX - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * contentScale / 2f,
  4968	            top = firstFieldTop - firstFieldY * contentScale,
  4969	            scale = contentScale
  4970	        )
  4971	    }

  4979	        if (!isPortraitIntroLayout()) {

  4986	        val formTop = stage.top + formTopY * stage.scale - portraitAuthScrollY

  4994	    private fun portraitAuthViewportTopPx(stage: StageMetrics): Float {
  4995	        val title = when (activeMockFlow) {
  4996	            MockAccountFlow.SignIn -> ACCOUNT_PROMPT_SIGN_IN_TEXT
  4997	            MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount -> ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CREATE_TEXT
  4998	            MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword -> ACCOUNT_PROMPT_FORGOT_TEXT
  4999	            null -> null
  5000	        }
  5001	        if (title != null) {
  5002	            val titleTextSize = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5003	                ACCOUNT_PROMPT_TEXT_SIZE * PORTRAIT_ACTION_SCALE * stage.scale
  5004	            } else {
  5005	                MOCK_FORM_TITLE_TEXT_SIZE * stage.scale
  5006	            }
  5007	            val titleBaseline = mockFormTitleTargetBaseline(
  5008	                title,
  5009	                mockPrimaryFormY(),
  5010	                stage.top,
  5011	                stage.scale,
  5012	                titleTextSize
  5013	            )
  5014	            formTitlePaint.textSize = titleTextSize
  5015	            val contentScale = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) portraitCreateContentScale() else 1f
  5016	            val titleToViewportGap = if (
  5017	                activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount && !isPortraitIntroLayout()
  5018	            ) {
  5019	                MOCK_FORM_TITLE_OPTICAL_BOTTOM_OFFSET
  5020	            } else {
  5021	                mockFormFieldGap()
  5022	            }
  5023	            return titleBaseline + formTitlePaint.fontMetrics.descent +
  5024	                titleToViewportGap * stage.scale * contentScale
  5025	        }
  5026	        return stage.top +
  5027	            (portraitLogoVisibleBottom() + MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_VIEWPORT_GAP) * stage.scale
  5028	    }

  5038	    private fun portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage: StageMetrics): Float {
  5039	        val stickyBottomHeight = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5040	            MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_VIEWPORT_BOTTOM_RESERVE *
  5041	                MOCK_PORTRAIT_STICKY_BOTTOM_SCALE * stage.scale
  5042	        } else {
  5043	            MOCK_LANDSCAPE_STICKY_BOTTOM_RESERVE * stage.scale
  5044	        }
  5045	        return height - systemBarInsetBottomPx -
  5046	            stickyBottomHeight * portraitStickyBottomMotion()
  5047	    }

  5074	    private fun portraitCreateMaxScrollY(
  5075	        stage: StageMetrics,
  5076	        yOffset: Float,
  5077	        focusShiftY: Float
  5078	    ): Float {
  5079	        val contentStage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(stage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  5080	        val avatarSectionY = mockCreateAvatarSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  5081	        val avatarBottomY = mockCreateAvatarBounds(
  5082	            mockCreateAvatarColumnX(),
  5083	            avatarSectionY
  5084	        ).let { it.y + it.h }
  5085	        val finalControlBottomY = maxOf(
  5086	            mockDropdownY(MockDropdown.Year, yOffset, focusShiftY) +
  5087	                mockDropdownControlHeight(MockDropdown.Year),
  5088	            mockDropdownY(MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier, yOffset, focusShiftY) +
  5089	                mockDropdownControlHeight(MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier)
  5090	        )
  5091	        val continueButtonBottomY = mockCreateContinueButtonY(yOffset, focusShiftY) +
  5092	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_HEIGHT
  5093	        val contentBottomY = maxOf(avatarBottomY, finalControlBottomY, continueButtonBottomY)
  5094	        val contentBottom = contentStage.top + contentBottomY * contentStage.scale +
  5095	            MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_CONTENT_BOTTOM_GAP * stage.scale + portraitAuthScrollY
  5096	        return (contentBottom - portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage)).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  5097	    }

  5149	    private fun mockCreateLandscapeFormTopY(yOffset: Float, focusShiftY: Float): Float {
  5150	        val avatarSectionY = mockCreateAvatarSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY) -
  5151	            mockCreatePhoneLandscapeFirstAppearanceShiftY()
  5152	        val heroBasedTop = mockCreateUsernameBaselineY(avatarSectionY) +
  5153	            MOCK_CREATE_LANDSCAPE_ROW_GAP
  5154	        val stage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return heroBasedTop
  5155	        val viewportBottomY = (portraitAuthViewportBottomPx(stage) - stage.top) / stage.scale
  5156	        return maxOf(
  5157	            heroBasedTop,
  5158	            viewportBottomY + MOCK_CREATE_LANDSCAPE_NEXT_SECTION_VIEWPORT_GAP
  5159	        )
  5160	    }
  5161	
  5162	    private fun mockCreateLandscapeHeadingBaselineY(
  5163	        yOffset: Float,
  5164	        focusShiftY: Float
  5165	    ): Float {
  5166	        val avatarSectionY = mockCreateAvatarSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  5167	        val avatarBounds = mockCreateAvatarBounds(
  5168	            mockCreateAvatarColumnX(),
  5169	            avatarSectionY
  5170	        )
  5171	        val avatarCenterY = avatarBounds.y + avatarBounds.h / 2f
  5172	        formTitlePaint.textSize = MOCK_CREATE_LANDSCAPE_HEADING_TEXT_SIZE *
  5173	            mockCreateLandscapeFirstImpressionScale()
  5174	        val textPath = Path()
  5175	        val textBounds = RectF()
  5176	        var blockTop = Float.MAX_VALUE
  5177	        var blockBottom = -Float.MAX_VALUE
  5178	        MOCK_CREATE_LANDSCAPE_HEADING_TEXT.split('\n').forEachIndexed { index, line ->
  5179	            textPath.reset()
  5180	            formTitlePaint.getTextPath(line, 0, line.length, 0f, 0f, textPath)
  5181	            textPath.computeBounds(textBounds, true)
  5182	            val lineOffsetY = index * MOCK_CREATE_LANDSCAPE_HEADING_LINE_HEIGHT *
  5183	                mockCreateLandscapeFirstImpressionScale()
  5184	            blockTop = minOf(blockTop, textBounds.top + lineOffsetY)
  5185	            blockBottom = maxOf(blockBottom, textBounds.bottom + lineOffsetY)
  5186	        }
  5187	        val headlineCenterFromFirstBaseline = (blockTop + blockBottom) / 2f
  5188	        return avatarCenterY - headlineCenterFromFirstBaseline
  5189	    }

  5277	    private fun mockForgotPasswordBodyBottomY(formTopY: Float): Float {
  5278	        val paragraphs = mockForgotPasswordBodyText().split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n"))
  5279	        val copyWidth = mockForgotPasswordCopyWidth()
  5280	        var bottomY = mockParagraphBottomY(
  5281	            paragraphs.firstOrNull().orEmpty(),
  5282	            formTopY + mockForgotPasswordBodyTopGap(),
  5283	            copyWidth,
  5284	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_TEXT_SIZE,
  5285	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LEAD_LINE_HEIGHT,
  5286	            forceLineBreaks = true
  5287	        )
  5288	        paragraphs.getOrNull(1)?.let { middleText ->
  5289	            bottomY = mockParagraphBottomY(
  5290	                middleText,
  5291	                bottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  5292	                copyWidth,
  5293	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  5294	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT
  5295	            )
  5296	        }
  5297	        val bottomText = paragraphs.drop(2).joinToString("\n\n")
  5298	        if (bottomText.isNotEmpty()) {
  5299	            bottomY = mockParagraphBottomY(
  5300	                bottomText,
  5301	                bottomY + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_LINE_HEIGHT,
  5302	                copyWidth,
  5303	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_TEXT_SIZE,
  5304	                MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BODY_MIDDLE_LINE_HEIGHT
  5305	            )
  5306	        }
  5307	        return bottomY
  5308	    }
  5309	
  5310	    private fun mockForgotPasswordBodyTopGap(): Float {
  5311	        return if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {

  5320	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_BUTTON_HEIGHT + MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_HELP_TOP_GAP
  5321	        return mockParagraphBottomY(
  5322	            mockForgotPasswordHelpText(),
  5323	            helpTopY,
  5324	            mockForgotPasswordFormWidth(),
  5325	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_HELP_TEXT_SIZE,
  5326	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_HELP_LINE_HEIGHT
  5327	        )

  5329	
  5330	    private fun mockParagraphBottomY(
  5331	        text: String,
  5332	        topY: Float,
  5333	        maxWidth: Float,
  5334	        textSize: Float,
  5335	        lineHeight: Float,
  5336	        forceLineBreaks: Boolean = false
  5337	    ): Float {
  5338	        formInputPaint.textSize = textSize
  5339	        val lineCount = if (forceLineBreaks) {
  5340	            text.split('\n').sumOf { line ->
  5341	                wrappedTextLines(line, maxWidth, formInputPaint).size
  5342	            }
  5343	        } else {
  5344	            wrappedTextLines(text, maxWidth, formInputPaint).size
  5345	        }
  5346	        return topY + lineCount * lineHeight
  5347	    }

  5349	    private fun mockForgotRecoverySelectorLayout(
  5350	        left: Float,
  5351	        width: Float,
  5352	        labelWidth: Float,
  5353	        boxWidth: Float
  5354	    ): ForgotRecoverySelectorLayout {
  5355	        val gap = ((width - labelWidth - boxWidth * 2f) / 4f).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  5356	        val labelX = left + gap
  5357	        val usernameBoxX = labelX + labelWidth + gap
  5358	        val passwordBoxX = usernameBoxX + boxWidth + gap
  5359	        return ForgotRecoverySelectorLayout(
  5360	            labelX = labelX,
  5361	            usernameBoxX = usernameBoxX,
  5362	            passwordBoxX = passwordBoxX,
  5363	            boxWidth = boxWidth
  5364	        )
  5365	    }
  5366	
  5367	    private fun mockForgotRecoverySelectorBoxWidth(
  5368	        usernameTextWidth: Float,
  5369	        passwordTextWidth: Float,
  5370	        baseBoxWidth: Float = MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SELECTOR_BOX_WIDTH
  5371	    ): Float {
  5372	        val widestTextWidth = maxOf(usernameTextWidth, passwordTextWidth)
  5373	        val currentHorizontalPadding = ((baseBoxWidth - widestTextWidth) / 2f).coerceAtLeast(0f)
  5374	        return widestTextWidth + currentHorizontalPadding * 4f
  5375	    }

  5389	    private fun mockCreateLandscapeFieldVisualScale(field: MockSignInField): Float {
  5390	        val isScaledCreateField = field == MockSignInField.Username ||
  5391	            field == MockSignInField.Password ||
  5392	            field == MockSignInField.Email ||
  5393	            field == MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword ||
  5394	            field == MockSignInField.ConfirmEmail
  5395	        return if (
  5396	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  5397	            !isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  5398	            isScaledCreateField

  5406	    private fun mockActiveFieldX(field: MockSignInField): Float {
  5407	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) return mockForgotPasswordFormX()
  5408	        if (activeMockFlow != MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) return mockFormX()
  5409	
  5410	        val firstColumnX = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) mockCreatePortraitColumnX() else mockCreateFormX()
  5411	        val middleColumnX = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5412	            mockCreatePortraitColumnX()
  5413	        } else {
  5414	            mockCreateLandscapeSecondColumnX()
  5415	        }
  5416	        val enlargedFieldOutwardShift = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5417	            0f
  5418	        } else {
  5419	            MOCK_FORM_WIDTH *
  5420	                (mockCreateLandscapeFirstImpressionScale() - 1f) / 2f
  5421	        }
  5422	        return when (field) {
  5423	            MockSignInField.Username -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5424	                firstColumnX
  5425	            } else {
  5426	                mockCreateLandscapeHeroLeftColumnX()
  5427	            }
  5428	            MockSignInField.Password,
  5429	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> firstColumnX - enlargedFieldOutwardShift
  5430	            MockSignInField.Email,
  5431	            MockSignInField.ConfirmEmail -> middleColumnX + enlargedFieldOutwardShift
  5432	            MockSignInField.Month -> mockDropdownX(MockDropdown.Month)
  5433	            MockSignInField.Day -> mockDropdownX(MockDropdown.Day)
  5434	            MockSignInField.Year -> mockDropdownX(MockDropdown.Year)
  5435	        }
  5436	    }

  5438	    private fun mockActiveFieldY(field: MockSignInField, yOffset: Float, focusShiftY: Float): Float {
  5439	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  5440	            return mockForgotPasswordFieldY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  5441	        }
  5442	        if (
  5443	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  5444	            !isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  5445	            field == MockSignInField.Username
  5446	        ) {
  5447	            return mockCreateLandscapeUsernameFieldY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  5448	        }
  5449	
  5450	        val usesSecondSection = activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount &&
  5451	            isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  5452	            field in listOf(
  5453	                MockSignInField.Email,
  5454	                MockSignInField.Month,
  5455	                MockSignInField.Day,
  5456	                MockSignInField.Year
  5457	            )
  5458	        val sectionTop = if (usesSecondSection) {
  5459	            mockCreateSecondSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  5460	        } else if (
  5461	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount && !isPortraitIntroLayout()
  5462	        ) {
  5463	            mockCreateLandscapeFormTopY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  5464	        } else {
  5465	            mockPrimaryFormY() + yOffset + focusShiftY
  5466	        }
  5467	        val fieldOffsetY = if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.CreateAccount) {
  5468	            when (field) {
  5469	                MockSignInField.Username -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5470	                    mockCreatePortraitUsernameOffsetY()
  5471	                } else {
  5472	                    0f
  5473	                }
  5474	                MockSignInField.Email -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5475	                    0f
  5476	                } else {
  5477	                    MOCK_CREATE_LANDSCAPE_PRIMARY_ROW_SHIFT_DOWN
  5478	                }
  5479	                MockSignInField.ConfirmEmail -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5480	                    0f
  5481	                } else {
  5482	                    mockCreateLandscapeRowOffset(1)
  5483	                }
  5484	                MockSignInField.Password -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5485	                    mockCreatePortraitPasswordOffsetY()
  5486	                } else {
  5487	                    MOCK_CREATE_LANDSCAPE_PRIMARY_ROW_SHIFT_DOWN
  5488	                }
  5489	                MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5490	                    mockCreatePortraitPasswordOffsetY() +
  5491	                        MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + mockFormFieldGap()
  5492	                } else {
  5493	                    mockCreateLandscapeRowOffset(1)
  5494	                }
  5495	                MockSignInField.Month,
  5496	                MockSignInField.Day,
  5497	                MockSignInField.Year -> mockCreateDateRowOffsetY()
  5498	            }
  5499	        } else {
  5500	            activeMockFields().indexOf(field).coerceAtLeast(0) *
  5501	                (MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + mockFormFieldGap())
  5502	        }
  5503	        return sectionTop + fieldOffsetY
  5504	    }
  5505

  5507	        return row.coerceAtLeast(0) *
  5508	            (MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + MOCK_CREATE_LANDSCAPE_ROW_GAP)
  5509	    }

  5530	    private fun mockBackChevronRowTopY(): Float {
  5531	        if (usesStickyAuthChrome()) {
  5532	            val stickyBottomHeight = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5533	                MOCK_CREATE_PORTRAIT_VIEWPORT_BOTTOM_RESERVE * MOCK_PORTRAIT_STICKY_BOTTOM_SCALE
  5534	            } else {
  5535	                MOCK_LANDSCAPE_STICKY_BOTTOM_RESERVE
  5536	            }
  5537	            val chevronHeight = MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_HEIGHT * if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5538	                MOCK_PORTRAIT_STICKY_BOTTOM_CHEVRON_SCALE
  5539	            } else {
  5540	                1f
  5541	            }
  5542	            val safeBottom = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5543	                portraitSafeBottom()
  5544	            } else {
  5545	                val stage = currentStageMetrics()
  5546	                if (stage == null) {
  5547	                    FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT
  5548	                } else {
  5549	                    (height - systemBarInsetBottomPx - stage.top) / stage.scale
  5550	                }
  5551	            }
  5552	            val settledTop = safeBottom - stickyBottomHeight +
  5553	                (stickyBottomHeight - chevronHeight) / 2f
  5554	            return settledTop + stickyBottomHeight * (1f - portraitStickyBottomMotion())
  5555	        }
  5556	        return ACCOUNT_PROMPT_CENTER_Y + signInStackShiftY() + signInAccountPromptExtraShiftY() +
  5557	            MOCK_BACK_CHEVRON_ROW_TOP_GAP
  5558	    }
  5559	
  5560	    private fun portraitStickyBottomMotion(): Float {
  5561	        if (!usesStickyAuthChrome()) return 0f
  5562	        return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  5563	    }

  5573	    private fun mockDropdownX(dropdown: MockDropdown): Float {

  5575	            mockCreatePortraitColumnX()

  5577	            mockCreateFormX()

  5580	            mockCreatePortraitColumnX()
  5581	        } else {
  5582	            mockCreateFormX() + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH + MOCK_CREATE_FORM_COLUMN_GAP
  5583	        }
  5584	        return when (dropdown) {
  5585	            MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5586	                middleColumnX
  5587	            } else {
  5588	                firstColumnX - mockCreateBirthdayOutwardShift() * 2f
  5589	            }
  5590	            MockDropdown.Month -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5591	                middleColumnX
  5592	            } else {

  5594	            }
  5595	            MockDropdown.Day -> middleColumnX
  5596	            MockDropdown.Year -> middleColumnX + mockDropdownWidth(MockDropdown.Day) +
  5597	                if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5598	                    mockCreateBirthdayFieldGap()
  5599	                } else {
  5600	                    MOCK_CREATE_FORM_COLUMN_GAP
  5601	                }
  5602	        }
  5603	    }

  5605	    private fun mockDropdownY(dropdown: MockDropdown, yOffset: Float, focusShiftY: Float): Float {
  5606	        val dropdownOffsetY = when (dropdown) {
  5607	            MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier -> if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5608	                MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT + mockFormFieldGap() * 2f
  5609	            } else {
  5610	                mockCreateLandscapeRowOffset(3)
  5611	            }
  5612	            MockDropdown.Month,
  5613	            MockDropdown.Day,
  5614	            MockDropdown.Year -> mockCreateDateRowOffsetY()
  5615	        }
  5616	        val sectionTop = if (isPortraitIntroLayout()) {
  5617	            mockCreateSecondSectionY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  5618	        } else {
  5619	            mockCreateLandscapeFormTopY(yOffset, focusShiftY)
  5620	        }
  5621	        val verticalCenterCorrection = if (mockDropdownOpensUp(dropdown)) {
  5622	            (mockDropdownControlHeight(dropdown) - MOCK_FORM_FIELD_HEIGHT) / 2f
  5623	        } else {
  5624	            0f
  5625	        }
  5626	        return sectionTop + dropdownOffsetY - verticalCenterCorrection
  5627	    }

  5653	    private fun mockDropdownWidth(dropdown: MockDropdown): Float {

  5655	            !isPortraitIntroLayout() &&
  5656	            dropdown in listOf(MockDropdown.Month, MockDropdown.Day, MockDropdown.Year)

  5660

  5662	            MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier -> MOCK_FORM_WIDTH
  5663	            MockDropdown.Month -> MOCK_DATE_MONTH_WIDTH
  5664	            MockDropdown.Day -> MOCK_DATE_DAY_WIDTH
  5665	            MockDropdown.Year -> MOCK_DATE_YEAR_WIDTH
  5666	        }
  5667	        return if (
  5668	            mockDropdownOpensUp(dropdown) ||
  5669	            (dropdown == MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier && !isPortraitIntroLayout())
  5670	        ) {
  5671	            baseWidth * mockCreateBirthdayVisualScale()
  5672	        } else {
  5673	            baseWidth
  5674	        }
  5675	    }

  5733	    private fun mockDropdownOptionsY(dropdown: MockDropdown, yOffset: Float, focusShiftY: Float): Float {

  5737	                mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown)

  5739	            controlY - mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown) *
  5740	                mockDropdownOptionHeight(dropdown)
  5741	        } else {
  5742	            controlY + mockDropdownControlHeight(dropdown)
  5743	        }
  5744	    }

  5812	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  5813	        val optionCount = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size
  5814	        val viewportCount = if (mockDropdownUsesDateViewport(dropdown)) {
  5815	            MOCK_DATE_DROPDOWN_VISIBLE_OPTION_COUNT
  5816	        } else {
  5817	            optionCount
  5818	        }
  5819	        return min(optionCount, viewportCount)
  5820	    }

  5822	    private fun mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown: MockDropdown): Boolean {
  5823	        return mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size > mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)
  5824	    }
