  5826	    private fun mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  5827	        return (mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] ?: 0).coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  5828	    }
  5829	
  5830	    private fun mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown): Int {
  5831	        return (mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).size - mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown)).coerceAtLeast(0)
  5832	    }
  5833	
  5834	    private fun setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown: MockDropdown, offset: Int) {
  5835	        mockDropdownScrollOffsets[dropdown] = offset.coerceIn(0, mockDropdownMaxScrollOffset(dropdown))
  5836	    }
  5837	
  5838	    private fun mockDropdownVisibleOptions(dropdown: MockDropdown): List<String> {
  5839	        val options = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown)
  5840	        val offset = mockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown)
  5841	        return options.drop(offset).take(mockDropdownVisibleOptionCount(dropdown))
  5842	    }
  5843	
  5844	    private fun prepareMockDropdownScroll(dropdown: MockDropdown) {
  5845	        if (!mockDropdownCanScroll(dropdown)) {
  5846	            setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, 0)
  5847	            return
  5848	        }
  5849	
  5850	        val selectedIndex = mockDropdownOptions(dropdown).indexOf(mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown))
  5851	        setMockDropdownScrollOffset(dropdown, if (selectedIndex >= 0) selectedIndex else 0)
  5852	    }
  5853	
  5854	    private fun mockDropdownDisplayText(dropdown: MockDropdown): String {
  5855	        return when (dropdown) {
  5856	            MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier -> normalizedMembershipTierSelection(subscriptionTierText)
  5857	            else -> mockDropdownSelectedValue(dropdown)
  5858	        }
  5859	    }
  5860

  5863	            MockDropdown.SubscriptionTier -> normalizedMembershipTierSelection(subscriptionTierText)
  5864	            MockDropdown.Month -> monthText
  5865	            MockDropdown.Day -> dayText
  5866	            MockDropdown.Year -> yearText
  5867	        }
  5868	    }

  5891	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()

  5981	    private fun mockCreateAccountValidationMessage(): String? {
  5982	        val normalizedEmail = emailText.trim()
  5983	        return when {
  5984	            usernameText.trim().length < 3 -> "Username needs at least 3 characters"
  5985	            passwordText.length < 8 -> "Password needs at least 8 characters"
  5986	            passwordText != confirmPasswordText -> "Passwords do not match"
  5987	            !normalizedEmail.contains('@') ||
  5988	                !normalizedEmail.substringAfterLast('@', "").contains('.') -> {
  5989	                "Enter a valid email address"
  5990	            }
  5991	            !isPortraitIntroLayout() && confirmEmailText.trim() != normalizedEmail -> {
  5992	                "Email addresses do not match"
  5993	            }
  5994	            subscriptionTierText == MOCK_FORM_SUBSCRIPTION_TIER_TEXT -> {
  5995	                "Select a subscription tier"
  5996	            }
  5997	            monthText == MOCK_FORM_MONTH_TEXT ||
  5998	                dayText == MOCK_FORM_DAY_TEXT ||
  5999	                yearText == MOCK_FORM_YEAR_TEXT -> {
  6000	                "Select your complete birthday"
  6001	            }
  6002	            else -> null
  6003	        }
  6004	    }

  6006	    private fun startMockForgotPasswordSubmission() {
  6007	        if (!mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()) {
  6008	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  6009	            return
  6010	        }
  6011	
  6012	        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  6013	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading
  6014	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  6015	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  6016	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  6017	    }

  6019	    private fun mockForgotPasswordSubmissionProgress(): Float {
  6020	        val startMillis = forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis ?: return if (
  6021	            forgotPasswordSubmissionState == MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  6022	        ) {
  6023	            1f
  6024	        } else {
  6025	            0f
  6026	        }
  6027	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  6028	        return (elapsed / MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SUBMIT_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  6029	    }

  6033	    }

  6035	    private fun finishMockForgotPasswordSubmissionIfNeeded() {
  6036	        val startMillis = forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis ?: return
  6037	        if (forgotPasswordSubmissionState != MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Loading) {
  6038	            forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null
  6039	            return
  6040	        }
  6041	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_SUBMIT_MS) return
  6042	
  6043	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Sent
  6044	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null
  6045	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  6046	    }

  6051	        forgotPasswordSubmissionState = MockForgotPasswordSubmissionState.Idle
  6052	        forgotPasswordSubmissionStartMillis = null
  6053	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  6054	    }

  6154	    private fun mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(): Float {
  6155	        val baseStage = currentStageMetrics() ?: return 0f
  6156	        val progress = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockSignInProgress())
  6157	        val yOffset = MOCK_FORM_ENTRY_Y * (1f - progress)
  6158	        val focusShiftY = mockFormFocusShiftY(FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockFormFocusProgress))
  6159	        val stage = mockCreateContentStageMetrics(baseStage, yOffset, focusShiftY)
  6160	        val scaledAvatarSize = MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_SIZE * MOCK_CREATE_AVATAR_STACK_SCALE *
  6161	            mockCreateFirstImpressionScale()
  6162	        return mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(mockCreateAvatarSlideDistance(scaledAvatarSize), stage.scale)
  6163	    }
  6164	
  6165	    private fun mockCreateAvatarDragProgress(slideDistance: Float, stageScale: Float): Float {
  6166	        val slideDistancePx = (slideDistance * stageScale).coerceAtLeast(1f)
  6167	        return (abs(mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX) / slideDistancePx).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  6168	    }

  6212	                mockFieldLabelFloatProgress[field] ?: 0f,

  6311	    private fun mockLandscapeInputLiftTarget(): Float {
  6312	        if (width <= height) return 0f
  6313	        if (mockSignInStartMillis == null || isMockSignInClosing()) return 0f
  6314	        return if (
  6315	            focusedMockField != null ||
  6316	            expandedMockDropdown != null ||
  6317	            isSoftKeyboardVisible()
  6318	        ) {
  6319	            1f
  6320	        } else {
  6321	            0f
  6322	        }
  6323	    }
  6324	
  6325	    private fun mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale: Float): Float {
  6326	        if (width <= height || mockSignInStartMillis == null) return 0f
  6327	        val liftMotion = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(mockLandscapeInputLiftProgress)
  6328	        return MOCK_FORM_LANDSCAPE_FOCUS_SHIFT_Y * stageScale * liftMotion
  6329	    }
  6330	
  6331	    private fun isSoftKeyboardVisible(): Boolean {
  6332	        val rootInsets = ViewCompat.getRootWindowInsets(this)
  6333	        if (rootInsets != null) {
  6334	            val imeInsets = rootInsets.getInsets(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime())
  6335	            if (rootInsets.isVisible(WindowInsetsCompat.Type.ime()) || imeInsets.bottom > 0) return true
  6336	        }
  6337	
  6338	        val rootHeight = rootView?.height ?: 0
  6339	        if (rootHeight <= 0) return false
  6340	        getWindowVisibleDisplayFrame(tempWindowRect)
  6341	        val obscuredHeight = rootHeight - tempWindowRect.height()
  6342	        return obscuredHeight > rootHeight * 0.18f
  6343	    }

  6431	            MockSignInField.Email -> emailText

  6439	    private fun setMockSignInFieldText(field: MockSignInField, text: String) {
  6440	        val limitedText = text.take(MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS)
  6441	        val usernameWasEmpty = usernameText.isEmpty()
  6442	        when (field) {
  6443	            MockSignInField.Username -> usernameText = limitedText
  6444	            MockSignInField.Password -> passwordText = limitedText
  6445	            MockSignInField.ConfirmPassword -> confirmPasswordText = limitedText
  6446	            MockSignInField.Email -> emailText = limitedText
  6447	            MockSignInField.ConfirmEmail -> confirmEmailText = limitedText
  6448	            MockSignInField.Month -> monthText = limitedText
  6449	            MockSignInField.Day -> dayText = limitedText
  6450	            MockSignInField.Year -> yearText = limitedText
  6451	        }
  6452	        if (field == MockSignInField.Username && usernameWasEmpty != usernameText.isEmpty()) {
  6453	            restartMockCreateUsernameRevealAnimation()
  6454	        }
  6455	        if (activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword && field == MockSignInField.Email) {
  6456	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  6457	        }
  6458	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  6459	        if (mockInputDimAwaitingField == field) {
  6460	            mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
  6461	        }
  6462	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  6463	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  6464	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  6465	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  6466	    }
  6467	
  6468	    private fun appendMockSignInInput(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  6469	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  6470	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  6471	        if (input.isEmpty()) return true
  6472	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  6473	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  6474	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  6475	        val insertedText = input.take(availableChars)
  6476	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + insertedText)
  6477	        activeComposingText = ""
  6478	        return true
  6479	    }
  6480	
  6481	    private fun replaceMockSignInComposingText(rawInput: String): Boolean {
  6482	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  6483	        val input = sanitizeMockSignInInput(rawInput)
  6484	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  6485	        val baseText = currentText.dropLast(activeComposingText.length.coerceAtMost(currentText.length))
  6486	        val availableChars = (MOCK_FORM_INPUT_MAX_CHARS - baseText.length).coerceAtLeast(0)
  6487	        val composingText = input.take(availableChars)
  6488	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, baseText + composingText)
  6489	        activeComposingText = composingText
  6490	        return true
  6491	    }
  6492	
  6493	    private fun finishMockSignInComposingText(): Boolean {
  6494	        activeComposingText = ""
  6495	        return true
  6496	    }
  6497	
  6498	    private fun deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength: Int): Boolean {
  6499	        val field = focusedMockField ?: return false
  6500	        val currentText = mockSignInFieldText(field)
  6501	        if (currentText.isEmpty()) {
  6502	            activeComposingText = ""
  6503	            return true
  6504	        }
  6505	        val deleteCount = beforeLength.coerceAtLeast(1).coerceAtMost(currentText.length)
  6506	        activeComposingText = ""
  6507	        setMockSignInFieldText(field, currentText.dropLast(deleteCount))
  6508	        return true
  6509	    }
  6510	
  6511	    private fun handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  6512	        activeComposingText = ""
  6513	        val fields = activeMockFields()
  6514	        val fieldIndex = fields.indexOf(focusedMockField)
  6515	        if (fieldIndex >= 0 && fieldIndex < fields.lastIndex && actionCode != EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE) {
  6516	            focusMockSignInField(fields[fieldIndex + 1])
  6517	        } else if (
  6518	            activeMockFlow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword &&
  6519	            focusedMockField == MockSignInField.Email &&
  6520	            mockForgotPasswordCanSubmit()
  6521	        ) {
  6522	            startMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  6523	        } else {
  6524	            val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  6525	            inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  6526	            clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  6527	        }
  6528	        return true
  6529	    }
  6530	
  6531	    private fun handleMockSignInKeyEvent(keyCode: Int, event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  6532	        if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  6533	        return when (keyCode) {
  6534	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  6535	            KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  6536	            else -> {
  6537	                val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  6538	                typedCharacter?.let(::appendMockSignInInput) ?: false
  6539	            }
  6540	        }
  6541	    }
  6542	
  6543	    private fun sanitizeMockSignInInput(input: String): String {
  6544	        return buildString {
  6545	            input.forEach { character ->
  6546	                if (character != '\n' && character != '\r' && !character.isISOControl()) {
  6547	                    append(character)
  6548	                }
  6549	            }
  6550	        }
  6551	    }
  6552	
  6553	    private fun trailingFittingText(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): String {
  6554	        if (paint.measureText(text) <= maxWidth) return text
  6555	
  6556	        var startIndex = 0
  6557	        while (startIndex < text.length && paint.measureText(text.substring(startIndex)) > maxWidth) {
  6558	            startIndex += 1
  6559	        }
  6560	        return text.substring(startIndex)
  6561	    }
  6562	
  6563	    private fun wrappedTextLines(text: String, maxWidth: Float, paint: Paint): List<String> {
  6564	        val lines = mutableListOf<String>()
  6565	        text.split(Regex("\\n\\s*\\n")).forEachIndexed { paragraphIndex, paragraph ->
  6566	            if (paragraphIndex > 0) {
  6567	                lines += ""
  6568	            }
  6569	
  6570	            var currentLine = ""
  6571	            paragraph.replace('\n', ' ')
  6572	                .split(Regex("\\s+"))
  6573	                .filter { it.isNotEmpty() }
  6574	                .forEach { word ->
  6575	                    val candidateLine = if (currentLine.isEmpty()) word else "$currentLine $word"
  6576	                    if (currentLine.isEmpty() || paint.measureText(candidateLine) <= maxWidth) {
  6577	                        currentLine = candidateLine
  6578	                    } else {
  6579	                        lines += currentLine
  6580	                        currentLine = word
  6581	                    }
  6582	                }
  6583	            if (currentLine.isNotEmpty()) {
  6584	                lines += currentLine
  6585	            }
  6586	        }
  6587	        return lines
  6588	    }
  6589	
  6590	    private fun moveToward(current: Float, target: Float, amount: Float): Float {
  6591	        return when {
  6592	            current < target -> (current + amount).coerceAtMost(target)
  6593	            current > target -> (current - amount).coerceAtLeast(target)
  6594	            else -> current
  6595	        }
  6596	    }

  6598	    private fun currentStageMetrics(): StageMetrics? {
  6599	        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return null
  6600	
  6601	        val standardScale = min(width / FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH, height / FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT)
  6602	        val portraitScale = portraitDesignScale()
  6603	        val portraitAmount = portraitIntroAmount()
  6604	        val stageScale = lerpFloat(standardScale, portraitScale, portraitAmount)
  6605	        val standardTop = (height - FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT * standardScale) / 2f
  6606	        return StageMetrics(
  6607	            left = (width - FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * stageScale) / 2f,
  6608	            top = lerpFloat(standardTop, 0f, portraitAmount) +
  6609	                mockAuthOpenStageLiftY(stageScale) +
  6610	                mockLandscapeInputStageLiftY(stageScale),
  6611	            scale = stageScale
  6612	        )
  6613	    }

  6623	    private fun portraitDesignScale(): Float {
  6624	        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0) return 1f

  6628	
  6629	        val referenceDesignHeight = FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH * PORTRAIT_REFERENCE_HEIGHT_TO_WIDTH
  6630	        return min(widthScale, height / referenceDesignHeight)
  6631	    }

  6749	        val logoHeight = LOGO_FINAL_HEIGHT * PORTRAIT_LOGO_SCALE
  6750	        val rawLogoTop = portraitStackCenter(1f / 6f) - logoHeight / 2f

  6764	            (rawForgotCenter + PORTRAIT_PROMPT_VISIBLE_HALF_HEIGHT)

  6771	            (PORTRAIT_COMFORTABLE_DESIGN_HEIGHT - PORTRAIT_COMPACT_DESIGN_HEIGHT))

  6773	    }

  6823	
  6824	    private fun phoneLandscapeWelcomeY(y: Float): Float {
  6825	        val scale = phoneLandscapeWelcomeAssetScale()
  6826	        val frameCenterY = FIGMA_FRAME_HEIGHT / 2f
  6827	        return frameCenterY + (y - frameCenterY) * scale
  6828	    }
  6829	
  6830	    private fun phoneLandscapeWelcomeBounds(bounds: Bounds): Bounds {
  6831	        val scale = phoneLandscapeWelcomeAssetScale()
  6832	        if (scale == 1f) return bounds
  6833	
  6834	        val centerX = bounds.x + bounds.w / 2f
  6835	        val centerY = phoneLandscapeWelcomeY(bounds.y + bounds.h / 2f)
  6836	        val scaledWidth = bounds.w * scale
  6837	        val scaledHeight = bounds.h * scale
  6838	        return Bounds(
  6839	            x = centerX - scaledWidth / 2f,
  6840	            y = centerY - scaledHeight / 2f,
  6841	            w = scaledWidth,
  6842	            h = scaledHeight
  6843	        )
  6844	    }
  6845	
  6846	    private fun accountPromptCreateCenterX(): Float {
  6847	        val originalThreeColumnWidth = MOCK_CREATE_FORM_COLUMNS * MOCK_FORM_WIDTH +
  6848	            (MOCK_CREATE_FORM_COLUMNS - 1) * MOCK_CREATE_FORM_COLUMN_GAP
  6849	        return (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - originalThreeColumnWidth) / 2f + MOCK_FORM_WIDTH / 2f
  6850	    }
  6851	
  6852	    private fun accountPromptForgotCenterX(): Float {
  6853	        return (FIGMA_FRAME_WIDTH - MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_USABLE_WIDTH) / 2f +
  6854	            MOCK_FORGOT_PASSWORD_USABLE_WIDTH * 0.8f
  6855	    }
  6856	
  6857	    private fun currentIntroSnapshot(): CinerificIntroSnapshot {
  6858	        val activeFlowName = activeMockFlow
  6859	            ?.takeIf { mockSignInStartMillis != null && mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress > 0f }
  6860	            ?.name
  6861	            .orEmpty()
  6862	        return CinerificIntroSnapshot(
  6863	            activeFlowName = activeFlowName,
  6864	            usernameText = usernameText,
  6865	            passwordText = passwordText,
  6866	            confirmPasswordText = confirmPasswordText,
  6867	            emailText = emailText,
  6868	            confirmEmailText = confirmEmailText,
  6869	            monthText = monthText,
  6870	            dayText = dayText,
  6871	            yearText = yearText,
  6872	            subscriptionTierText = normalizedMembershipTierSelection(subscriptionTierText),
  6873	            forgotRecoveryTargetName = forgotRecoveryTarget.name,
  6874	            forgotPasswordSubmissionStateName = forgotPasswordSubmissionState.name,
  6875	            rememberMeChecked = rememberMeChecked,
  6876	            createAvatarIndex = mockCreateAvatarIndex,
  6877	            portraitProfileIndex = portraitProfileIndex
  6878	        )
  6879	    }

  6881	    private fun notifyIntroSnapshotChanged() {
  6882	        val snapshot = currentIntroSnapshot()
  6883	        if (appliedIntroSnapshot == snapshot) return
  6884	
  6885	        appliedIntroSnapshot = snapshot
  6886	        onIntroSnapshotChanged?.invoke(snapshot)
  6887	    }

  6889	    private fun switchMockAccountFlow(flow: MockAccountFlow) {
  6890	        if (
  6891	            mockSignInStartMillis == null ||
  6892	            activeMockFlow == flow ||
  6893	            isMockSignInClosing() ||
  6894	            isMockFlowSwitchAnimating()
  6895	        ) {
  6896	            return
  6897	        }
  6898	
  6899	        val previousFlow = activeMockFlow ?: return
  6900	        clearMockSignInFieldFocus()
  6901	        activeComposingText = ""
  6902	        expandedMockDropdown = null
  6903	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  6904	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  6905	        portraitAuthScrollY = 0f
  6906	        portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
  6907	        pressedMockField = null
  6908	        pressedRememberMe = false
  6909	        pressedBackChevron = false
  6910	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  6911	        activeMockCreateAvatarDrag = false
  6912	        mockCreateAvatarDragMoved = false
  6913	        mockCreateAvatarDragDeltaX = 0f
  6914	        lockedMockCreateAvatarDragDirection = null
  6915	        pressedMockDropdown = null
  6916	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  6917	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  6918	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  6919	        pressedCreateAccountContinue = false
  6920	        showCreateAccountValidationWarning = false
  6921	        resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()
  6922	        if (flow == MockAccountFlow.ForgotPassword) {
  6923	            forgotRecoveryTarget = MockForgotRecoveryTarget.Password
  6924	            emailText = ""
  6925	            confirmEmailText = ""
  6926	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  6927	        }
  6928	        outgoingMockFlow = previousFlow
  6929	        activeMockFlow = flow
  6930	        mockFlowSwitchStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  6931	        restartMockFormLabelAnimation()
  6932	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()
  6933	        restartMockLandscapeInputLiftAnimation()
  6934	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  6935	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  6936	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  6937	    }
  6938	
  6939	    private fun openMockSignInScreen() {
  6940	        if (mockSignInStartMillis != null && mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 1f) return
  6941	        val currentProgress = mockSignInProgress()
  6942	        activeMockFlow = MockAccountFlow.SignIn
  6943	        mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  6944	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = currentProgress
  6945	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
  6946	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  6947	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  6948	    }

  6958	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = currentProgress

  6986	        pressedBackChevron = false
  6987	        pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  6988	        pressedMockDropdown = null
  6989	        pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  6990	        pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  6991	        pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  6992	        pressedCreateAccountContinue = false
  6993	        showCreateAccountValidationWarning = false
  6994	        activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  6995	        mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  6996	        portraitAuthScrollY = 0f
  6997	        portraitAuthScrollMoved = false
  6998	        activeComposingText = ""
  6999	        mockInputDimAwaitingField = null
  7000	        usernameText = ""
  7001	        passwordText = ""

  7006	        dayText = MOCK_FORM_DAY_TEXT

  7020	        restartMockFormFocusAnimation()

  7022	        restartMockInputDimAnimation()
  7023	        val inputMethodManager = context.getSystemService(Context.INPUT_METHOD_SERVICE) as? InputMethodManager
  7024	        inputMethodManager?.hideSoftInputFromWindow(windowToken, 0)
  7025	        clearFocus()
  7026	        mockSignInStartMillis = SystemClock.uptimeMillis()
  7027	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = currentProgress
  7028	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
  7029	        notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  7030	        postInvalidateOnAnimation()

  7032	
  7033	    private fun mockSignInProgress(): Float {
  7034	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return 0f
  7035	        val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis
  7036	        val progress = (elapsed / mockSignInTransitionDurationMs().toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  7037	        return lerpFloat(
  7038	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress,
  7039	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress,
  7040	            progress
  7041	        )
  7042	    }
  7043	
  7044	    private fun mockFlowSwitchProgress(): Float {
  7045	        val startMillis = mockFlowSwitchStartMillis ?: return 1f
  7046	        return ((SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis) / MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS.toFloat())
  7047	            .coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  7048	    }
  7049	
  7050	    private fun isMockFlowSwitchAnimating(): Boolean {
  7051	        return mockFlowSwitchStartMillis != null && mockFlowSwitchProgress() < 1f
  7052	    }
  7053	
  7054	    private fun finishMockFlowSwitchIfNeeded() {
  7055	        if (mockFlowSwitchStartMillis == null || mockFlowSwitchProgress() < 1f) return
  7056	        outgoingMockFlow = null
  7057	        mockFlowSwitchStartMillis = null
  7058	    }
  7059	
  7060	    private fun isMockSignInAnimating(): Boolean {
  7061	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return false
  7062	        return SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()
  7063	    }
  7064	
  7065	    private fun isMockSignInClosing(): Boolean {
  7066	        return mockSignInStartMillis != null && mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f
  7067	    }
  7068	
  7069	    private fun mockSignInTransitionDurationMs(): Long {
  7070	        val distance = abs(mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress - mockSignInTransitionStartProgress)
  7071	        return (MOCK_SIGN_IN_TRANSITION_MS * distance)
  7072	            .roundToInt()
  7073	            .coerceAtLeast(1)
  7074	            .toLong()
  7075	    }
  7076	
  7077	    private fun finishMockSignInTransitionIfNeeded() {
  7078	        val startMillis = mockSignInStartMillis ?: return
  7079	        if (SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - startMillis < mockSignInTransitionDurationMs()) return
  7080	
  7081	        if (mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress == 0f) {
  7082	            mockSignInStartMillis = null
  7083	            activeMockFlow = null
  7084	            mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 0f
  7085	            mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 0f
  7086	            pressedMockField = null
  7087	            pressedRememberMe = false
  7088	            pressedBackChevron = false
  7089	            pressedMockCreateAvatarNav = null
  7090	            pressedMockDropdown = null
  7091	            pressedMockDropdownOption = null
  7092	            pressedForgotRecoveryTarget = null
  7093	            pressedForgotPasswordSubmit = false
  7094	            pressedCreateAccountContinue = false
  7095	            activeMockDropdownScroll = null
  7096	            mockDropdownScrollMoved = false
  7097	            mockDragReturnInProgress = false
  7098	            resetMockForgotPasswordSubmission()
  7099	            resetMockCreateAvatarCarousel()
  7100	            notifyIntroSnapshotChanged()
  7101	            postInvalidateOnAnimation()
  7102	            return
  7103	        }
  7104	
  7105	        mockSignInTransitionStartProgress = 1f
  7106	        mockSignInTransitionTargetProgress = 1f
  7107	    }
  7108	
  7109	    private inner class MockSignInInputConnection : BaseInputConnection(this@CinerificIntroView, false) {
  7110	        override fun commitText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {
  7111	            return appendMockSignInInput(text?.toString().orEmpty())
  7112	        }
  7113	
  7114	        override fun setComposingText(text: CharSequence?, newCursorPosition: Int): Boolean {
  7115	            return replaceMockSignInComposingText(text?.toString().orEmpty())
  7116	        }
  7117	
  7118	        override fun finishComposingText(): Boolean {
  7119	            return finishMockSignInComposingText()
  7120	        }
  7121	
  7122	        override fun deleteSurroundingText(beforeLength: Int, afterLength: Int): Boolean {
  7123	            return deleteMockSignInInput(beforeLength)
  7124	        }
  7125	
  7126	        override fun deleteSurroundingTextInCodePoints(beforeLength: Int, afterLength: Int): Boolean {
  7127	            return deleteSurroundingText(beforeLength, afterLength)
  7128	        }
  7129	
  7130	        override fun getTextBeforeCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
  7131	            val text = focusedMockField?.let(::mockSignInFieldText).orEmpty()
  7132	            return text.takeLast(n.coerceAtLeast(0))
  7133	        }
  7134	
  7135	        override fun getTextAfterCursor(n: Int, flags: Int): CharSequence {
  7136	            return ""
  7137	        }
  7138	
  7139	        override fun getSelectedText(flags: Int): CharSequence {
  7140	            return ""
  7141	        }
  7142	
  7143	        override fun performEditorAction(actionCode: Int): Boolean {
  7144	            return handleMockSignInEditorAction(actionCode)
  7145	        }
  7146	
  7147	        override fun sendKeyEvent(event: KeyEvent): Boolean {
  7148	            if (event.action != KeyEvent.ACTION_DOWN) return true
  7149	            return when (event.keyCode) {
  7150	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_DEL -> deleteMockSignInInput(1)
  7151	                KeyEvent.KEYCODE_ENTER -> handleMockSignInEditorAction(EditorInfo.IME_ACTION_DONE)
  7152	                else -> {
  7153	                    val typedCharacter = event.unicodeChar.takeIf { it != 0 }?.toChar()?.toString()
  7154	                    if (typedCharacter != null) {
  7155	                        appendMockSignInInput(typedCharacter)
  7156	                    } else {
  7157	                        super.sendKeyEvent(event)
  7158	                    }
  7159	                }
  7160	            }
  7161	        }
  7162	    }
  7163	}

  7335	private inline fun <reified T : Enum<T>> enumValueOrNull(name: String): T? {
  7336	    return runCatching { enumValueOf<T>(name) }.getOrNull()
  7337	}
  7338	
  7339	private fun bootProgressAt(bootStartMillis: Long): Float {
  7340	    val elapsed = SystemClock.uptimeMillis() - bootStartMillis
  7341	    return (elapsed / BOOT_ANIMATION_MS.toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  7342	}
  7343	
  7344	private fun linearSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  7345	    val ms = progress.coerceIn(0f, 1f) * BOOT_ANIMATION_MS
  7346	    return ((ms - startMs) / (endMs - startMs).toFloat()).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  7347	}
  7348	
  7349	private fun easedSegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  7350	    return FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  7351	}
  7352	
  7353	private fun bouncySegmentMs(progress: Float, startMs: Int, endMs: Int): Float {
  7354	    return bouncyEasing(linearSegmentMs(progress, startMs, endMs))
  7355	}
  7356	
  7357	private fun bouncyEasing(value: Float): Float {
  7358	    val t = value.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  7359	    val base = FastOutSlowInEasing.transform(t)
  7360	    val bounce = sin(t * PI.toFloat() * 4.5f) * (1f - t) * 0.13f
  7361	    return base + bounce
  7362	}
  7363	
  7364	private fun lerpFloat(start: Float, end: Float, amount: Float) = start + (end - start) * amount
  7365	
  7366	private fun lerpBounds(start: Bounds, end: Bounds, amount: Float) = Bounds(
  7367	    x = lerpFloat(start.x, end.x, amount),
  7368	    y = lerpFloat(start.y, end.y, amount),
  7369	    w = lerpFloat(start.w, end.w, amount),
  7370	    h = lerpFloat(start.h, end.h, amount)
  7371	)
  7372	
  7373	private fun offsetBounds(bounds: Bounds, yOffset: Float) = Bounds(
  7374	    x = bounds.x,
  7375	    y = bounds.y + yOffset,
  7376	    w = bounds.w,
  7377	    h = bounds.h
  7378	)
  7379	
  7380	private fun scaleBoundsFromTopCenter(bounds: Bounds, scale: Float): Bounds {
  7381	    val scaledWidth = bounds.w * scale
  7382	    return Bounds(
  7383	        x = bounds.x + (bounds.w - scaledWidth) / 2f,
  7384	        y = bounds.y,
  7385	        w = scaledWidth,
  7386	        h = bounds.h * scale
  7387	    )
  7388	}
  7389	
  7390	private fun lerpColor(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  7391	    val t = amount.coerceIn(0f, 1f)
  7392	    return Color.argb(
  7393	        lerpInt(Color.alpha(start), Color.alpha(end), t),
  7394	        lerpInt(Color.red(start), Color.red(end), t),
  7395	        lerpInt(Color.green(start), Color.green(end), t),
  7396	        lerpInt(Color.blue(start), Color.blue(end), t)
  7397	    )
  7398	}
  7399	
  7400	private fun lerpInt(start: Int, end: Int, amount: Float): Int {
  7401	    return (start + (end - start) * amount).roundToInt()
  7402	}
  7403	
  7404	private fun combineBitmaps(base: Bitmap, overlay: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  7405	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(base.width, base.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  7406	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  7407	    val paint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  7408	    canvas.drawBitmap(base, 0f, 0f, paint)
  7409	    canvas.drawBitmap(overlay, 0f, 0f, paint)
  7410	    return output
  7411	}
  7412	
  7413	private fun circularBitmap(source: Bitmap): Bitmap {
  7414	    val output = Bitmap.createBitmap(source.width, source.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
  7415	    val canvas = Canvas(output)
  7416	    val rect = RectF(0f, 0f, source.width.toFloat(), source.height.toFloat())
  7417	    val maskPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)
  7418	    val imagePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG or Paint.FILTER_BITMAP_FLAG or Paint.DITHER_FLAG)
  7419	    canvas.drawOval(rect, maskPaint)
  7420	    imagePaint.xfermode = PorterDuffXfermode(PorterDuff.Mode.SRC_IN)
  7421	    canvas.drawBitmap(source, null, rect, imagePaint)
  7422	    imagePaint.xfermode = null
  7423	    return output
  7424	}

    31	    )
    32	}

============================================================
FILE: README.md
============================================================

     1	# Cinerific
     2	
     3	Local Android project for the `prahlin/cinerific` GitHub repo.
     4	
     5	This is a first Kotlin / Jetpack Compose scaffold based on the Figma intro and sign-in frames. The current app opens as a tablet-friendly full-screen frame preview with:
     6	
     7	- Intro frame 1
     8	- Animated intro frame 2
     9	- Promo/logo intro frame 3
    10	- Sign-in/loading frame 4
    11	
    12	## Stack
    13	
    14	- Kotlin
    15	- Jetpack Compose Material 3
    16	- Android Gradle Plugin 8.5.x
    17	- Gradle 8.7
    18	
    19	## Run it
    20	
    21	Open the project in Android Studio and run `app`, or use the Gradle wrapper:
    22	
    23	```bash
    24	./gradlew :app:assembleDebug
    25	```
    26	
    27	The project pins Gradle to Android Studio's bundled JBR in `gradle.properties`, so command-line builds do not accidentally use an unsupported system JDK.
    28	
    29	From VS Code, run the default build task `Android: assemble debug`. There are also tasks for `Android: install debug` and `Android: launch Cinerific` when an emulator or device is connected.
    30	
    31	### Standard tablet emulator
    32	
    33	Use the guarded launcher for the persistent low-load QA profile:
    34	
    35	```bash
    36	./start_cinerific_android_standard.sh
    37	```
    38	
    39	The launcher requests and verifies `1280x800` at `160 dpi`, two guest CPU cores,
    40	host GPU acceleration, and a frameless display with direct host-pointer mapping
    41	before opening Cinerific. It refuses to continue if the running AVD does not match.
    42	Emulator logs are written to
    43	`~/Library/Logs/Cinerific/standard-emulator.log`.
    44	
    45	## Notes for the first Kotlin pass
    46	
    47	- The layout is intentionally simple and adaptive for tablets.
    48	- The current visuals are Compose approximations of the Figma frames and are meant to be replaced with production assets screen by screen.
    49	- The local project folder is the same path as the GitHub remote: `prahlin/cinerific`.

============================================================
FILE: start_cinerific_android_standard.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	set -Eeuo pipefail
     3	
     4	ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
     5	SDK="${ANDROID_HOME:-${ANDROID_SDK_ROOT:-$HOME/Library/Android/sdk}}"
     6	EMU="${SDK}/emulator/emulator"
     7	ADB="${SDK}/platform-tools/adb"
     8	AVD="Cinerific_Standard_Tablet"
     9	AVD_CONFIG="$HOME/.android/avd/${AVD}.avd/config.ini"
    10	DEVICE="emulator-5554"
    11	PORT="5554"
    12	APP_PACKAGE="com.prahlin.cinerific"
    13	APP_ACTIVITY="${APP_PACKAGE}/.MainActivity"
    14	EXPECTED_SIZE="1280x800"
    15	EXPECTED_DENSITY="160"
    16	EXPECTED_CORES="2"
    17	EXPECTED_FRAME="no"
    18	BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS="${BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS:-180}"
    19	LOG_DIR="$HOME/Library/Logs/Cinerific"
    20	LOG="$LOG_DIR/standard-emulator.log"
    21	ERROR_LOG="$LOG_DIR/standard-emulator.err.log"
    22	LAUNCHD_LABEL="com.cinerific.standardavd"
    23	LAUNCHD_PLIST="$ROOT/launchd/${LAUNCHD_LABEL}.plist"
    24	
    25	fail() {
    26	  printf 'ERROR: %s\n' "$*" >&2
    27	  exit 1
    28	}
    29	
    30	[[ -x "$EMU" ]] || fail "Android emulator not found at $EMU"
    31	[[ -x "$ADB" ]] || fail "adb not found at $ADB"
    32	[[ -f "$LAUNCHD_PLIST" ]] || fail "LaunchAgent not found at $LAUNCHD_PLIST"
    33	[[ -f "$AVD_CONFIG" ]] || fail "AVD configuration not found at $AVD_CONFIG"
    34	"$EMU" -list-avds | grep -qx "$AVD" || fail "AVD $AVD was not found"
    35	
    36	config_frame="$(sed -n 's/^showDeviceFrame=//p' "$AVD_CONFIG")"
    37	[[ "$config_frame" == "$EXPECTED_FRAME" ]] || fail "Expected a frameless AVD, got showDeviceFrame=$config_frame"
    38	
    39	mkdir -p "$LOG_DIR"
    40	
    41	device_is_connected() {
    42	  "$ADB" devices | awk -v device="$DEVICE" \
    43	    '$1 == device && $2 == "device" { found = 1 } END { exit !found }'
    44	}
    45	
    46	if device_is_connected; then
    47	  actual_avd="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" emu avd name 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r' | sed -n '1p')"
    48	  [[ "$actual_avd" == "$AVD" ]] || fail "$DEVICE is running $actual_avd, not $AVD"
    49	  printf '%s is already running.\n' "$AVD"
    50	else
    51	  printf 'Starting %s with the low-load QA profile...\n' "$AVD"
    52	  launchctl bootout "gui/$(id -u)/${LAUNCHD_LABEL}" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
    53	  : > "$LOG"
    54	  : > "$ERROR_LOG"
    55	  launchctl bootstrap "gui/$(id -u)" "$LAUNCHD_PLIST"
    56	fi
    57	
    58	deadline=$((SECONDS + BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS))
    59	until device_is_connected; do
    60	  (( SECONDS < deadline )) || fail "Emulator did not connect within ${BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS}s; see $LOG"
    61	  sleep 2
    62	done
    63	
    64	until [[ "$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell getprop sys.boot_completed 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r')" == "1" ]]; do
    65	  (( SECONDS < deadline )) || fail "Android did not finish booting within ${BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS}s; see $LOG"
    66	  sleep 2
    67	done
    68	
    69	actual_avd="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" emu avd name 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r' | sed -n '1p')"
    70	actual_size="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell wm size | sed -n 's/^Physical size: //p' | tr -d '\r')"
    71	actual_density="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell wm density | sed -n 's/^Physical density: //p' | tr -d '\r')"
    72	actual_cores="$("$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell 'grep -c "^processor" /proc/cpuinfo' | tr -d '\r')"
    73	
    74	[[ "$actual_avd" == "$AVD" ]] || fail "Expected $AVD, got $actual_avd"
    75	[[ "$actual_size" == "$EXPECTED_SIZE" ]] || fail "Expected $EXPECTED_SIZE, got $actual_size"
    76	[[ "$actual_density" == "$EXPECTED_DENSITY" ]] || fail "Expected density $EXPECTED_DENSITY, got $actual_density"
    77	[[ "$actual_cores" == "$EXPECTED_CORES" ]] || fail "Expected $EXPECTED_CORES cores, got $actual_cores"
    78	
    79	sleep 10
    80	device_is_connected || fail "Emulator disconnected after boot; see $LOG"
    81	
    82	if "$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell pm path "$APP_PACKAGE" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    83	  "$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell am force-stop "$APP_PACKAGE"
    84	  "$ADB" -s "$DEVICE" shell am start -n "$APP_ACTIVITY" >/dev/null
    85	  printf 'Cinerific launched on %s.\n' "$DEVICE"
    86	else
    87	  printf 'Cinerific is not installed on %s; emulator verification still passed.\n' "$DEVICE"
    88	fi
    89	
    90	printf 'Verified: %s @ %s dpi, %s cores, host GPU.\n' \
    91	  "$actual_size" "$actual_density" "$actual_cores"
    92	printf 'Emulator log: %s\n' "$LOG"


============================================================
FILE: read_project.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	
     3	PROJECT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
     4	OUTPUT_FILE="$PROJECT_DIR/cinerific_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt"
     5	
     6	echo "Generating Cinerific project snapshot..."
     7	echo "Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR"
     8	echo "Snapshot: $OUTPUT_FILE"
     9	echo ""
    10	
    11	write_header() {
    12	  cat > "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
    13	Cinerific Android PROJECT SNAPSHOT
    14	Generated: $(date)
    15	Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR
    16	
    17	IMPORTANT INSTRUCTIONS FOR CHATGPT & CODEX:
    18	1. Read this entire snapshot before giving code edits.
    19	2. Preserve all assets, spacing, alignment, fonts, images, layout, animations, and relative positioning unless specifically asked to change them.
    20	3. Do not remove unrelated code.
    21	4. Do not rewrite large sections unless requested.
    22	5. When giving code changes, include exact line/row numbers whenever possible.
    23	6. Print full scripts when requested.
    24	7. Treat this project structure as the source of truth.
    25	8. If you don't understand something, ask for clarification instead of making assumptions.
    26	9. Warn me, or at least caution me, if I ask for something that would break the project or cause it to not work as intended.
    27	
    28	
    29	============================================================
    30	SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
    31	============================================================
    32	
    33	This file is rebuilt from the current Cinerific workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
    34	Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.
    35	
    36	The snapshot intentionally ignores:
    37	- .git
    38	- .gradle
    39	- .idea
    40	- app/build
    41	- build
    42	- .kotlin
    43	- .tmp
    44	- .cache
    45	- .DS_Store
    46	- local.properties
    47	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
    48	- *.jks
    49	- *.keystore
    50	
    51	Binary image and screenshot files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.
    52	
    53	
    54	============================================================
    55	PROJECT FILE TREE
    56	============================================================
    57	
    58	EOF
    59	}
    60	
    61	print_file() {
    62	  local file="$1"
    63	
    64	  if [ -f "$file" ]; then
    65	    {
    66	      echo ""
    67	      echo ""
    68	      echo "============================================================"
    69	      echo "FILE: ${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}"
    70	      echo "============================================================"
    71	      echo ""
    72	      nl -ba "$file"
    73	    } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    74	  fi
    75	}
    76	
    77	print_binary_inventory() {
    78	  local title="$1"
    79	  local dir="$2"
    80	
    81	  {
    82	    echo ""
    83	    echo ""
    84	    echo "============================================================"
    85	    echo "$title"
    86	    echo "============================================================"
    87	    echo ""
    88	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    89	
    90	  if [ ! -d "$dir" ]; then
    91	    echo "Directory not present: ${dir#$PROJECT_DIR/}" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    92	    return
    93	  fi
    94	
    95	  find "$dir" -type f \
    96	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
    97	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
    98	    ! -name "*.jks" \
    99	    ! -name "*.keystore" \
   100	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   101	    local size
   102	    local modified
   103	    size="$(stat -f "%z" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%s" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   104	    modified="$(stat -f "%Sm" -t "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%y" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   105	    printf '%s | %s bytes | modified %s\n' "${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}" "$size" "$modified" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   106	  done
   107	}
   108	
   109	write_snapshot() {
   110	  write_header
   111	
   112	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" \
   113	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git" -prune -o \
   114	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.gradle" -prune -o \
   115	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.idea" -prune -o \
   116	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build" -prune -o \
   117	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/build" -prune -o \
   118	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.kotlin" -prune -o \
   119	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.tmp" -prune -o \
   120	    -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.cache" -prune -o \
   121	    -name ".DS_Store" -prune -o \
   122	    -name "local.properties" -prune -o \
   123	    -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" -prune -o \
   124	    -name "*.jks" -prune -o \
   125	    -name "*.keystore" -prune -o \
   126	    -print | sort >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   127	
   128	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   129	
   130	
   131	============================================================
   132	FILE CONTENTS — ANDROID SOURCE
   133	============================================================
   134	
   135	EOF
   136	
   137	  for file in \
   138	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml" \
   139	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/MainActivity.kt" \
   140	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificApp.kt" \
   141	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/CinerificIntroView.kt" \
   142	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/java/com/prahlin/cinerific/ui/theme/Theme.kt" \
   143	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml" \
   144	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values/themes.xml" \
   145	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values-night/themes.xml" \
   146	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/values-v31/themes.xml" \
   147	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/splash_transparent.xml" \
   148	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher_background.xml" \
   149	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_launcher_foreground.xml" \
   150	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher.xml" \
   151	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/mipmap-anydpi-v26/ic_launcher_round.xml"
   152	  do
   153	    print_file "$file"
   154	  done
   155	
   156	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   157	
   158	
   159	============================================================
   160	FILE CONTENTS — ROOT / TOOLING
   161	============================================================
   162	
   163	EOF
   164	
   165	  for file in \
   166	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.gitignore" \
   167	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.github/copilot-instructions.md" \
   168	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.vscode/mcp.json" \
   169	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.vscode/tasks.json" \
   170	    "$PROJECT_DIR/README.md" \
   171	    "$PROJECT_DIR/build.gradle.kts" \
   172	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/build.gradle.kts" \
   173	    "$PROJECT_DIR/settings.gradle.kts" \
   174	    "$PROJECT_DIR/gradle.properties" \
   175	    "$PROJECT_DIR/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.properties" \
   176	    "$PROJECT_DIR/launchd/com.cinerific.standardavd.plist" \
   177	    "$PROJECT_DIR/start_cinerific_android_standard.sh" \
   178	    "$PROJECT_DIR/read_project.sh"
   179	  do
   180	    print_file "$file"
   181	  done
   182	
   183	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / IMAGE ASSET INVENTORY — DRAWABLE-NODPI" "$PROJECT_DIR/app/src/main/res/drawable-nodpi"
   184	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / IMAGE ASSET INVENTORY — EMULATOR SKINS" "$PROJECT_DIR/emulator/skins"
   185	  print_binary_inventory "BINARY / SCREENSHOT INVENTORY" "$PROJECT_DIR/screenshots"
   186	
   187	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   188	
   189	
   190	============================================================
   191	NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
   192	============================================================
   193	
   194	The Cinerific snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private directories:
   195	- .git
   196	- .gradle
   197	- .idea
   198	- app/build
   199	- build
   200	- .kotlin
   201	- .tmp
   202	- .cache
   203	- .DS_Store
   204	- local.properties
   205	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
   206	- *.jks
   207	- *.keystore
   208	
   209	The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
   210	
   211	EOF
   212	}
   213	
   214	write_snapshot
   215	
   216	echo "Done."
   217	echo ""
   218	echo "Snapshot created here:"
   219	echo "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   220	echo ""
   221	echo "Open it with:"
   222	echo "open \"$OUTPUT_FILE\""

============================================================
NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
============================================================

The Cinerific snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private directories:
- .git
- .gradle
- .idea
- app/build
- build
- .kotlin
- .tmp
- .cache
- .DS_Store
- local.properties
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.jks
- *.keystore

The snapshot lists binary assets and screenshots by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF bytes.
